# M12 plan: API snapshot, change report, name lock

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Every regeneration says what changed in the API of each client and how it affects code that uses it (R14.1): `generate` keeps an API snapshot of every client in `typed_openapi.lock` next to the config, prints one line of change counts per client in five categories, refuses a changed API with `--locked`, and keeps the names of types and methods it gave before, so adding a schema or an operation never renames an existing one; `typed_openapi diff` reports the changes in text, JSON or Markdown and fails CI on a chosen category; the config key `exhaustive: false` makes every enum, union, status type and event union an interface, so a new value does not break a `switch` (R14.2).

**Architecture:** The snapshot is built from the IR, the emitters' only input, so it is what the generated code declares, without reading that code and without `package:analyzer` (`lib/src/snapshot/`, task 3). A classifier with one rule table compares two snapshots (`lib/src/diff/`, task 4). `generate` reads and writes the lock through `ApiLockRun` (task 5); `diff` is a new command over the same pieces (task 6). The lock is also a name lock: `SnapshotNameLock` turns a snapshot into a `NameLock` that `IrBuilder` takes, and `NameScope` gives each kept name to its identity before any other claim (task 7). Task 1 makes room first, with byte-identical output, in three allowlisted units that M12 must change: `ir_builder.dart` (groups and method names), `config_loader.dart` (the readers) and `GenerateCommand.run` (writing a client). Task 8 runs the corpus, history and fuzz gates; task 9 documents the architecture and the roadmap verdicts.

**Tech Stack:** Dart `>=3.10.0 <4.0.0`, pure Dart; `package:args` for the `diff` command, `package:crypto` (already a dependency) for identities; tests on `package:test`; generated code analyzed with `dart analyze --fatal-infos` in a subprocess.

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-m12-evolution-design.md](../specs/2026-10-13-m12-evolution-design.md), decisions 1-7 (binding). Requirements: R14.1, R14.2 ([requirements.md](../../requirements.md)). Carried over: the M12 row and every row parked for M12 in [roadmap.md](../../roadmap.md), and the "Parked from the M9/M10 reviews" tables. Code rules and size limits: [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md), [code-quality-review.md](../../code-quality-review.md).

## Global Constraints

- SDK `>=3.10.0 <4.0.0`; every package pure Dart; primary constructors (3.13) are not used.
- Dart 3.10 is used actively: dot shorthands, null-aware elements, switch expressions and patterns, records, class modifiers. In tests the arguments of `expect(...)` use full names.
- OOP, no top-level functions except `main` in `bin/` and tests.
- The snapshot is built from the IR only: no `package:analyzer`, no reading of generated code, no network. The same spec, config and lock give the same snapshot and the same lock text on any machine (sorted keys, no absolute path: a spec location is relative to the directory of the root document).
- Generated output is byte-identical with the default config (`exhaustive: true`) and with a lock that keeps the names a fresh run gives: `corpus check` finds 0 differences after every task, and `corpus.lock` is not changed by M12.
- Errors in user input (a lock that cannot be read, a bad `exhaustive`) are diagnostics, never exceptions: TOA835 and TOA902.
- New diagnostic codes go into `DiagnosticCode` with a doc comment and a row in `doc/diagnostics.md`: the naming area gets the next free codes TOA569-TOA571, `generate` TOA834-TOA835 (both fixed codes).
- Size limits (CLAUDE.md, "Code quality rules"): a `lib/` file at most 300 lines, a test file 500, a method 40; the ratchet tests pass after every task. No allowlisted unit grows: task 1 moves code out of `ir_builder.dart`, `config_loader.dart` and `GenerateCommand.run` before any feature touches them, and they leave the allowlists.
- Every spec string in a report is neutralised (text) or escaped (Markdown) as the diagnostics reporters do; JSON keeps it as it is.
- `dart format lib test bin` changes 0 files on a second run in every package; `dart analyze packages` from the root prints `No issues found!`; no `// ignore:`.
- `dart test` and `dart format` run from the package directory; `dart pub get`, `dart analyze packages`, `git apply` and `git` from the root. New files are created from their blocks in full, a **Replace** block replaces a whole file, and diffs are applied with `git apply` from the root.
- Every commit is a Conventional Commit and ends with the `Co-Authored-By` line of the model that does the task (the examples use `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`).
- Documents are in English.

## Decisions taken

The spec's decisions are binding. These are the technical choices it leaves to the plan; choices users see are under "Open questions", each with the default this plan implements.

1. **Identities.** A schema is its location: the file relative to the directory of the root document (empty for the root document) and the JSON pointer, with `@input` or `@patch` for the request and PATCH models of a schema split by direction (`NameLock.declarationKey`). An operation is its `operationId` when no other operation of the client has it, else `METHOD /path`; the method that returns its events adds `#stream`; its status types are `<op>#success` and `<op>#error`, its event union `<op>#events`, its parts class `<op>#parts`. A group is `group:<key>` (the tag or path word, `''` for the default group), the root client `root`.
2. **What a snapshot holds.** Per type its Dart name, `TypeKind` (`model`, `enumeration`, `union`, `statusType`, `eventUnion`, `parts`, `group`, `root`), `exhaustive`, facts and members; per member its Dart name, `MemberKind` (`field`, `value`, `variant`, `statusCase`, `event`, `method`, `parameter`, `body`, `groupField`, `server`), the owner (the method of an argument) and facts (`type`, `required`, `json`, `const`, `doc`, `deprecated`, and for methods `method`, `path`, `returns`, `accept`, `responses`; for arguments `in`, `wire`, `style`, `explode`, `allowReserved`, `media`). A type is written as Dart code with declarations named by identity (`key`), so a renamed type changes the type facts of its users only through its own entry.
3. **The rule table.** `FactChanges`: `type`, `returns`, `kind`, `known`, `unknown`, `additional`, `additionalName`, `data`, `variables` and any fact the table does not know are `breaking`; `required` is `breaking` when it becomes true, else `additive`; `json`, `const`, `method`, `path`, `accept`, `responses`, `in`, `wire`, `style`, `explode`, `allowReserved`, `media`, `discriminator`, `values`, `url` are `wire`; `doc` and `deprecated` are `docs`. `MemberChanges`: a removed or renamed member is `breaking`; a new field or argument is `additive` when optional, `breaking` when required; a new value, variant, status case or event is `switchBreaking` on an exhaustive type and `additive` on an interface; a new method is `additive`. `ApiDiff`: a removed or renamed type is `breaking`, an added one `additive`, a changed kind `breaking`; a sealed type that becomes an interface is `switchBreaking` (a `switch` needs a default case now), an interface that becomes sealed `breaking` (a class that implements it no longer compiles). A test (`the table knows every fact the snapshot writes`) fails when the builder writes a fact the table does not list.
4. **The lock text.** `{"version": 1, "clients": {"<client>": {"<identity>": {"name", "kind", "exhaustive"?, "facts"?, "members"?}}}}` with sorted keys, a type's lines apart and each member on one line (`SnapshotJson`), so a change of the API is a readable change of the file. `exhaustive` is written only when false.
5. **The lock and the manifest.** `typed_openapi.lock` (next to the config, committed) records what the code declares; the manifest `.typed_openapi.json` (inside each output) records which files `generate` wrote, so it replaces only those. They are independent: deleting an output keeps the lock, and the manifest's inputs hash does not cover the lock. The output lock `.<output>.typed_openapi.lock` of M9 is a third, unrelated file.
6. **Names kept.** Models, enums, unions and methods (also event-stream methods) are kept: these are the names a spec change renames through a collision suffix or an inline name. Fields, enum values, arguments, group classes and the helper types (status types, event unions, parts classes, variant classes) are named from the spec: their scopes are per type or per operation, and a collision there is reported by `diff` as `breaking`.
7. **Every command names with the lock.** `SpecChecker` reads the lock next to the config (`ApiLockNames`), so `check`, `generate`, `diff`, `baseline` and `ir` see the same names; the `NameLock` is part of the run key. `SpecChecker.freshNames` (and `generate --fresh-names`) ignores it.
8. **Precedence of a kept name.** An override `name` and a custom type win over a kept name: the type it was kept for is named anew (TOA569, warning). A normal claim never takes a name kept for another identity, so the new type gets the suffix. A kept name a fresh run would not give is TOA570 (info), and a kept type the run does not declare is TOA571 (info): its name is free again.
9. **`exhaustive` in the run key.** `GenerationConfig.key` gains `exhaustive` only when it is false, so the key, and the inputs hash of the manifest, of a config without it is unchanged.

## New commands

```sh
dart run typed_openapi diff [-c typed_openapi.yaml] [--format text|json|markdown] [--fail-on breaking|switch-breaking|wire|any] [client...]
dart run typed_openapi diff --from base.lock [--to head.lock]   # two lock files, no spec read with --to
dart run typed_openapi generate --locked        # CI: the API must equal typed_openapi.lock (TOA834)
dart run typed_openapi generate --fresh-names   # name everything from the spec, ignore the kept names
```

## Interfaces between tasks

- Task 1 produces `GroupIr(name, operations, {field})` in `ir/group_ir.dart` (exported by `ir.dart`), `GroupNames(IrContext).choose(SpecOperation, Map<String, String> tagOfGroup) -> GroupChoice(name, {untagged})` and `className(...)`, `MethodNames(IrContext).of(...) -> Map<SpecOperation, String>`, `ConfigReader(file, diagnostics, spans)`, `GenerationReader(...).read() -> GenerationConfig`, `TypeSettingsReader(sources, invalid).numberType()`, and `ClientWriter(...).write(ClientCheck) -> String` (the line of a client).
- Task 2 produces `GenerationConfig.exhaustive` (bool, default true), `TypeSettingsReader.exhaustive()`, `StatusTypeIr(name, cases, {exhaustive = true})`, `EventUnionIr.exhaustive`, set by the instance method `named(..., {required bool exhaustive})`.
- Task 3 produces `GroupIr.key`, `SnapshotBuilder(Uri root).build(ClientIr) -> ApiSnapshot`, `ApiSnapshot(Map<String, SnapshotType> types)`, `SnapshotType(name, TypeKind, {exhaustive, facts, members})`, `SnapshotMember(name, MemberKind, {owner, facts})`, `SnapshotIdentities(ClientIr, Uri root)` (`location`, `declaration`, `operation`, `method`), and `SnapshotJson().encode(Map<String, ApiSnapshot>) -> String` / `decode(String) -> Map<String, ApiSnapshot>` (throws `FormatException`).
- Task 4 produces `enum ChangeCategory {breaking, switchBreaking, wire, additive, docs}` with `label` and `parse(String)`, `ApiChange(category, message, {type, member, fix})` with `toJson()`, and `ApiDiff().compare(ApiSnapshot before, ApiSnapshot after) -> List<ApiChange>` (sorted by category).
- Task 5 produces `ChangeCounts(Iterable<ApiChange>)` (`of`, `isEmpty`, `toString()` = `0 breaking, 0 switch-breaking, 0 wire, 0 additive, 0 docs`), `ApiLock(DiagnosticBag, DisplayPaths)` (`fileName`, `pathFor(configPath)`, `read(path)`, `write(path, clients)`), `ApiLockRun.open(...)`, `ApiLockRun.snapshotOf(ClientConfig, ClientIr)`, `admits`, `record`, `finish`, and the codes `DiagnosticCode.apiLockDiffers` (TOA834) and `apiLockUnusable` (TOA835).
- Task 6 produces `DiffCommand(out, err, [paths])` and `TextDiffReport`, `JsonDiffReport`, `MarkdownDiffReport` with `format(Map<String, List<ApiChange>>) -> String`.
- Task 7 produces `NameLock({root, types, methods})` with `none`, `locationKey`, `declarationKey`, `methodKey(method, path, {required isStream})`, `typeKey(DeclId)`; `NameScope.lock(Map<String, String>)` and `claim(candidate, {alternatives, identity, isExplicit}) -> (String, NameChange?)` with `NameChange {reserved, collision, kept, lockTaken}`; `LockedNames(IrContext)` (`lockTypes`, `claimType`, `lockMethods`, `claimMethod`); `IrContext(..., {names})`, `IrBuilder(bag, {names})`; `SnapshotNameLock.of(ApiSnapshot, Uri root) -> NameLock`; `ApiLockNames(bag, paths).of(ProjectConfig) -> Map<String, NameLock>`; `SnapshotBuilder.rootOf(ClientConfig) -> Uri`; `const SpecChecker.freshNames(bag, [paths])`; codes `lockedNameTaken` (TOA569), `lockedNameKept` (TOA570), `lockedNameReleased` (TOA571).

## Verification

Tasks 1-9 were verified: built and checked on a scratch branch, then replayed from `main` (`c3e0e26`) using only this plan's blocks (new files created whole, **Replace** blocks copied whole, diffs applied with `git apply`, each commit made by the task's own commit block); after each task `dart analyze packages` is clean, a second `dart format` changes 0 files, the size ratchets pass and `dart test` is green in every package with the counts given in the task; the final tree equals the verified one. Before M12: 1378 generator tests, 305 client tests, 54 Dio transport tests and 201 tool tests (`~1`/`~2` skips: the SARIF schema is not in the local cache). M12 adds tests to the generator only.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the refactor**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/client_writer.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';

import '../check/spec_checker.dart';
import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../emit/api_library.dart';
import '../generate/output_writer.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';

/// Writes each client a `generate` run checked into its output directory
/// and says in one line what became of it.
final class ClientWriter {
  /// Creates a writer that writes through [_writer]; [protected] are the
  /// files no output may hold, [overlapping] the clients whose output
  /// overlaps another's, and [force] and [breakLock] the flags of the run.
  const ClientWriter(
    this._writer,
    this._paths, {
    required this.protected,
    required this.overlapping,
    required this.force,
    required this.breakLock,
  });

  final OutputWriter _writer;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  /// Every file any client of the config reads, by absolute path.
  final List<String> protected;

  /// The names of the clients whose output overlaps that of another.
  final Set<String> overlapping;

  /// Whether files the generator did not write may be replaced.
  final bool force;

  /// Whether a lock that cannot be told may be taken over.
  final bool breakLock;

  /// Writes the client of [check], unless it has errors or an overlapping
  /// output; returns the line that says what happened.
  String write(ClientCheck check) {
    final client = check.client;
    final ir = check.ir;
    if (ir == null || check.errors > 0) {
      return 'Skipped ${client.name}: its check reports errors; fix them and '
          'run generate again.';
    }
    if (overlapping.contains(client.name)) {
      return 'Skipped ${client.name}: its output overlaps the output of '
          'another client (TOA904).';
    }
    final files = _files(client, ir);
    final changes = _writer.write(
      client,
      files,
      inputs: _inputsOf(check),
      protected: protected,
      force: force,
      breakLock: breakLock,
    );
    if (changes == null) return 'Skipped ${client.name}: see the error above.';
    return 'Generated ${client.name} in ${_paths.of(client.output)}: '
        '${files.length} files, ${changes.written} written, '
        '${changes.deleted} deleted.'
        '${changes.forced == 0 ? '' : ' --force replaced '
                  '${changes.forced} foreign or modified '
                  '${changes.forced == 1 ? 'file' : 'files'}.'}';
  }

  /// The files of the whole client [client] with the IR [ir].
  static Map<String, String> _files(ClientConfig client, ClientIr ir) =>
      ApiLibrary(width: client.lineWidth).files(ir, client: client.name);

  /// The hash of what the code of [check]'s client is generated from: the
  /// content of every file the run read, the client's settings that change
  /// the code, and the generator. The same inputs give the same hash on
  /// any machine: paths are not part of it.
  static String _inputsOf(ClientCheck check) {
    final client = check.client;
    String hashOf(String path) {
      try {
        return sha256.convert(File(path).readAsBytesSync()).toString();
      } on FileSystemException {
        return 'unreadable';
      }
    }

    return sha256
        .convert(
          utf8.encode(
            jsonEncode({
              'generator': OutputWriter.generator,
              'files': [for (final path in check.sources) hashOf(path)],
              'generation': client.generation.key,
              'line_width': client.lineWidth,
              'preset': client.preset?.name,
              'rules': client.disabledRules.toList()..sort(),
            }),
          ),
        )
        .toString();
  }
}
````

````dart
import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';
import 'package:io/io.dart' show ExitCode;

import '../check/spec_checker.dart';
import '../config/config_loader.dart';
import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../diagnostics/reporters.dart';
import '../generate/output_writer.dart';
import '../generate/real_paths.dart';
import 'check_command.dart';
import 'client_writer.dart';

/// `typed_openapi generate`: checks every client as `check` does and
/// writes each client without errors into its output directory: the
/// models, the API classes and the root client (M8, M9).
///
/// A client whose check reports an error, including an overrides file that
/// cannot be used, is skipped; so is one whose output directory the
/// generator may not replace (TOA830): a directory that holds a package, a
/// file the config makes any client read, or a file the generator did not
/// write or that changed since it did (`--force` replaces those and lists
/// each, never the others; `--break-lock` only takes over a lock). The report
/// and a line per client go to stdout, and the exit code is the one `check`
/// gives.
final class GenerateCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates the command; reports go to [_out], and files are named as
  /// [_paths] does.
  GenerateCommand(this._out, [this._paths = const DisplayPaths()]) {
    argParser
      ..addOption(
        'config',
        abbr: 'c',
        defaultsTo: 'typed_openapi.yaml',
        help: 'Path to the config file.',
      )
      ..addFlag(
        'force',
        negatable: false,
        help:
            'Replace files in an output directory that typed_openapi did not '
            'write or that changed since it wrote them; each is listed.',
      )
      ..addFlag(
        'break-lock',
        negatable: false,
        help:
            'Take over the lock of an output when it cannot be told whether '
            'the run that holds it is still going. Replaces no file.',
      );
  }

  final StringSink _out;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  @override
  String get name => 'generate';

  @override
  String get description =>
      'Check specs and generate the clients without errors.';

  @override
  String get invocation =>
      '${runner!.executableName} generate [options] [client...]';

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final results = argResults!;
    final diagnostics = DiagnosticBag();
    final configPath = results.option('config')!;
    final config = ConfigLoader(diagnostics, _paths).load(configPath);
    final lines = <String>[];
    if (config != null) {
      final only = results.rest.toSet();
      final unknown = only.difference(config.declaredClients);
      if (unknown.isNotEmpty) {
        usageException(
          'Unknown client: ${(unknown.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
        );
      }
      final checks = SpecChecker(diagnostics, _paths).check(config, only: only);
      final writer = ClientWriter(
        OutputWriter(diagnostics, _paths),
        _paths,
        protected: _protected(configPath, config, checks),
        overlapping: _overlapping(config.clients),
        force: results.flag('force'),
        breakLock: results.flag('break-lock'),
      );
      lines.addAll(checks.map(writer.write));
    }
    _out.writeln(const TextReporter().format(diagnostics));
    lines.forEach(_out.writeln);
    if (config == null) return ExitCode.config.code;
    return diagnostics.hasErrors
        ? CheckCommand.exitHasErrors
        : ExitCode.success.code;
  }

  /// Every file any client of [config] reads, also one that is not run, by
  /// absolute path: an output directory must not hold one, or the run would
  /// delete it.
  List<String> _protected(
    String configPath,
    ProjectConfig config,
    List<ClientCheck> checks,
  ) => {
    _paths.absolute(configPath),
    for (final client in config.clients) ...[
      client.input,
      ?client.overrides,
      ?client.baseline,
    ],
    for (final check in checks) ...check.sources,
  }.toList()..sort();

  /// The names of the clients whose output directory is, or is inside, that
  /// of another client, also through symbolic links or a different case.
  static Set<String> _overlapping(List<ClientConfig> clients) => {
    for (var i = 0; i < clients.length; i++)
      for (var j = i + 1; j < clients.length; j++)
        if (RealPaths.overlap(clients[i].output, clients[j].output)) ...[
          clients[i].name,
          clients[j].name,
        ],
  };
}
````

````dart
/// The keys `typed_openapi.yaml` allows in each of its sections; any other
/// key is reported (TOA902).
final class ConfigKeys {
  ConfigKeys._();

  /// The keys of the file itself.
  static const topLevel = {'defaults', 'clients'};

  /// The keys of how code is generated, allowed in `defaults` and in a
  /// client.
  static const generation = {
    'naming',
    'group_by',
    'include',
    'exclude',
    'types',
    'tri_state',
    'union',
    'errors',
    'sse',
  };

  /// The keys of `defaults`.
  static const defaults = {
    'preset',
    'line_width',
    'rules',
    'severity',
    ...generation,
  };

  /// The keys of a client.
  static const client = {
    'input',
    'output',
    'preset',
    'overrides',
    'baseline',
    'line_width',
    'rules',
    'severity',
    ...generation,
  };

  /// The keys of `naming`.
  static const naming = {
    'client',
    'group',
    'default_group',
    'input_suffix',
    'patch_suffix',
    'operation_prefix',
    'stream_suffix',
  };

  /// The keys of `types`.
  static const types = {'number', 'formats', 'schemas'};
}
````

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:source_span/source_span.dart';

import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import '../diagnostics/severity.dart';
import '../diagnostics/severity_levels.dart';
import '../generate/real_paths.dart';
import '../load/yaml_composer.dart';
import 'config_keys.dart';
import 'config_reader.dart';
import 'generation_reader.dart';
import 'project_config.dart';

/// Reads `typed_openapi.yaml` into a [ProjectConfig].
///
/// Problems go to the [DiagnosticBag] given to the constructor instead of
/// being thrown.
final class ConfigLoader {
  /// Creates a loader that reports to [_diagnostics]; [_paths] names the
  /// config file in diagnostics.
  const ConfigLoader(this._diagnostics, [this._paths = const DisplayPaths()]);

  final DiagnosticBag _diagnostics;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  /// Reads the config file at [path], relative to the working directory of
  /// the [DisplayPaths] given to the constructor.
  ///
  /// Relative paths inside it are resolved against its directory. Returns
  /// null when the file is missing or unusable; otherwise returns every
  /// client that is valid.
  ProjectConfig? load(String path) {
    final full = _paths.absolute(path);
    final file = _paths.of(path);
    final type = FileSystemEntity.typeSync(full);
    if (type == FileSystemEntityType.notFound) {
      _diagnostics.report(
        .configNotFound,
        'Config file $file does not exist.',
        file: file,
        fix: 'Create typed_openapi.yaml, or pass its path with --config.',
      );
      return null;
    }
    if (type == FileSystemEntityType.directory) {
      _diagnostics.report(
        .configInvalid,
        'Config path $file is a directory, not a file.',
        file: file,
        fix: 'Pass the path of typed_openapi.yaml itself with --config.',
      );
      return null;
    }
    final String text;
    try {
      text = File(full).readAsStringSync();
    } on FileSystemException catch (e) {
      _diagnostics.report(
        .configInvalid,
        'Cannot read $file: ${e.message}.',
        file: file,
      );
      return null;
    }
    final composed = _compose(text, file, full);
    if (composed == null) return null;
    final reader = ConfigReader(file, _diagnostics, composed.spans);
    final root = composed.root;
    if (root is! Map<String, Object?>) {
      reader.invalid('The config must be a mapping.', .root);
      return null;
    }
    reader.checkKeys(root, ConfigKeys.topLevel, .root);

    final defaultsPointer = JsonPointer.root.child('defaults');
    final defaults = reader.mapAt(root, 'defaults', .root);
    Preset? defaultPreset;
    int? defaultWidth;
    var defaultRules = const <String, bool>{};
    var defaultLevels = const <DiagnosticCode, Severity?>{};
    if (defaults != null) {
      reader.checkKeys(defaults, ConfigKeys.defaults, defaultsPointer);
      defaultPreset = reader.presetAt(defaults, defaultsPointer);
      defaultWidth = reader.intAt(defaults, 'line_width', defaultsPointer);
      defaultRules = reader.rulesAt(defaults, defaultsPointer);
      defaultLevels = reader.severityAt(defaults, defaultsPointer);
    }

    final clientsPointer = JsonPointer.root.child('clients');
    final clientNodes = reader.mapAt(root, 'clients', .root, required: true);
    if (clientNodes == null) return null;
    if (clientNodes.isEmpty) {
      reader.invalid('"clients" is empty.', clientsPointer);
      return null;
    }

    final baseDir = p.dirname(full);
    String resolve(String value) => p.normalize(p.join(baseDir, value));

    final clients = <ClientConfig>[];
    final declared = <String>{};
    final outputSpans = <String, SourceSpan?>{};
    final baselineSpans = <String, SourceSpan?>{};
    for (final MapEntry(key: name, :value) in clientNodes.entries) {
      declared.add(name);
      final pointer = clientsPointer.child(name);
      if (value is! Map<String, Object?>) {
        reader.invalid('Client "$name" must be a mapping.', pointer);
        continue;
      }
      reader.checkKeys(value, ConfigKeys.client, pointer);
      final input = reader.stringAt(value, 'input', pointer, required: true);
      final output = reader.stringAt(value, 'output', pointer, required: true);
      final preset = reader.presetAt(value, pointer) ?? defaultPreset;
      final lineWidth =
          reader.intAt(value, 'line_width', pointer) ??
          defaultWidth ??
          ClientConfig.defaultLineWidth;
      final overrides = reader.stringAt(value, 'overrides', pointer);
      final baseline = reader.stringAt(value, 'baseline', pointer);
      final rules = {...defaultRules, ...reader.rulesAt(value, pointer)};
      final levels = {
        ...defaultLevels,
        ...reader.severityAt(value, pointer),
      };
      final generation = GenerationReader(reader, [
        if (defaults != null) (defaults, defaultsPointer),
        (value, pointer),
      ]).read();
      if (input == null || output == null) continue;
      outputSpans[name] = reader.spanAt(pointer.child('output'));
      baselineSpans[name] = reader.spanAt(pointer.child('baseline'));
      clients.add(
        ClientConfig(
          name: name,
          input: resolve(input),
          output: resolve(output),
          lineWidth: lineWidth,
          preset: preset,
          overrides: overrides == null ? null : resolve(overrides),
          baseline: baseline == null ? null : resolve(baseline),
          disabledRules: {
            for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in rules.entries)
              if (!value) key,
          },
          severity: SeverityLevels(levels),
          generation: generation,
        ),
      );
    }
    clients.sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name));
    _checkOutputOverlap(clients, outputSpans, file);
    _checkSharedBaselines(clients, baselineSpans, file);
    return ProjectConfig(
      file: path,
      clients: clients,
      declaredClients: declared,
    );
  }

  /// Reads [text] into plain values; null after reporting why it cannot be
  /// used. Every problem of the YAML itself is a config error.
  ComposedYaml? _compose(String text, String file, String path) {
    final yaml = DiagnosticBag();
    final composed = YamlComposer(
      yaml,
    ).compose(text, file: file, uri: p.toUri(path));
    for (final d in yaml.all) {
      _diagnostics.report(
        .configInvalid,
        d.message,
        severity: d.severity,
        file: file,
        pointer: d.pointer,
        span: d.span,
        fix: d.fix,
      );
    }
    if (composed?.syntaxError case final error?) {
      _diagnostics.report(
        .configInvalid,
        error.message,
        file: file,
        span: error.span,
      );
      return null;
    }
    return composed;
  }

  /// Reports a baseline file named by two clients: each client's baseline
  /// is written from that client's diagnostics alone.
  void _checkSharedBaselines(
    List<ClientConfig> clients,
    Map<String, SourceSpan?> spans,
    String file,
  ) {
    final owners = <String, String>{};
    for (final client in clients) {
      final baseline = client.baseline;
      if (baseline == null) continue;
      final owner = owners[p.canonicalize(baseline)];
      if (owner == null) {
        owners[p.canonicalize(baseline)] = client.name;
        continue;
      }
      _diagnostics.report(
        .configInvalid,
        'Clients "$owner" and "${client.name}" name the same baseline file.',
        file: file,
        pointer: JsonPointer.root
            .child('clients')
            .child(client.name)
            .child('baseline'),
        span: spans[client.name],
        fix: 'Give each client its own baseline file.',
      );
    }
  }

  void _checkOutputOverlap(
    List<ClientConfig> clients,
    Map<String, SourceSpan?> outputSpans,
    String file,
  ) {
    for (var i = 0; i < clients.length; i++) {
      for (var j = i + 1; j < clients.length; j++) {
        final a = clients[i];
        final b = clients[j];
        if (!RealPaths.overlap(a.output, b.output)) continue;
        _diagnostics.report(
          .outputOverlap,
          'Clients "${a.name}" and "${b.name}" write to overlapping '
          'directories.',
          file: file,
          pointer: JsonPointer.root
              .child('clients')
              .child(b.name)
              .child('output'),
          span: outputSpans[b.name],
          fix: 'Give each client its own output directory.',
        );
      }
    }
  }
}
````

````dart
import 'package:source_span/source_span.dart';

import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import '../diagnostics/severity.dart';
import '../normalize/normalizer.dart';
import 'project_config.dart';

/// Typed access to the values of a config document that reports bad ones.
final class ConfigReader {
  /// Creates a reader of the config [file] that reports to [diagnostics];
  /// [spans] are the places of its values by JSON pointer.
  ConfigReader(this.file, this.diagnostics, this.spans);

  /// The config file, as diagnostics name it.
  final String file;

  /// Where problems go.
  final DiagnosticBag diagnostics;

  /// The places of the values, by JSON pointer.
  final Map<String, SourceSpan> spans;

  /// The place of the value at [at], if known.
  SourceSpan? spanAt(JsonPointer at) => spans[at.value];

  /// Reports every key of [map], at [at], that is not [allowed].
  void checkKeys(
    Map<String, Object?> map,
    Set<String> allowed,
    JsonPointer at,
  ) {
    for (final name in map.keys) {
      if (allowed.contains(name)) continue;
      diagnostics.report(
        .configUnknownKey,
        'Unknown key "$name".',
        file: file,
        pointer: at.child(name),
        span: spanAt(at.child(name)),
        fix: 'Allowed keys here: ${(allowed.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
      );
    }
  }

  /// The mapping under [key] of [parent] at [at]; null, reported, when it
  /// is not a mapping or [required] and missing.
  Map<String, Object?>? mapAt(
    Map<String, Object?> parent,
    String key,
    JsonPointer at, {
    bool required = false,
  }) {
    if (!parent.containsKey(key)) {
      if (required) invalid('Missing required key "$key".', at);
      return null;
    }
    final value = parent[key];
    if (value is Map<String, Object?>) return value;
    invalid('"$key" must be a mapping.', at.child(key));
    return null;
  }

  /// The non-empty string under [key] of [parent] at [at]; null, reported,
  /// when it is not one or [required] and missing.
  String? stringAt(
    Map<String, Object?> parent,
    String key,
    JsonPointer at, {
    bool required = false,
  }) {
    if (!parent.containsKey(key)) {
      if (required) invalid('Missing required key "$key".', at);
      return null;
    }
    final value = parent[key];
    if (value is String && value.isNotEmpty) return value;
    invalid('"$key" must be a non-empty string.', at.child(key));
    return null;
  }

  /// The positive integer under [key] of [parent] at [at]; null, reported,
  /// when it is not one.
  int? intAt(Map<String, Object?> parent, String key, JsonPointer at) {
    if (!parent.containsKey(key)) return null;
    final value = parent[key];
    if (value is int && value > 0) return value;
    invalid('"$key" must be a positive integer.', at.child(key));
    return null;
  }

  /// `rules` of [parent]: normalizer rule names mapped to whether they run.
  /// Unknown names are reported and skipped.
  Map<String, bool> rulesAt(Map<String, Object?> parent, JsonPointer at) {
    final rules = mapAt(parent, 'rules', at);
    if (rules == null) return const {};
    final pointer = at.child('rules');
    final known = Normalizer.ruleNames;
    final result = <String, bool>{};
    for (final MapEntry(key: name, :value) in rules.entries) {
      if (!known.contains(name)) {
        diagnostics.report(
          .configUnknownKey,
          'Unknown normalizer rule "$name".',
          file: file,
          pointer: pointer.child(name),
          span: spanAt(pointer.child(name)),
          fix: 'Known rules: ${(known.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
        );
        continue;
      }
      if (value is! bool) {
        invalid(
          'Rule "$name" must be true or false.',
          pointer.child(name),
        );
        continue;
      }
      result[name] = value;
    }
    return result;
  }

  /// `severity` of [parent]: diagnostic codes, by id or slug, mapped to
  /// their level; `null` is `off`. Unknown codes are a warning, bad levels
  /// and levels a code cannot have are errors; all of them are skipped.
  Map<DiagnosticCode, Severity?> severityAt(
    Map<String, Object?> parent,
    JsonPointer at,
  ) {
    final entries = mapAt(parent, 'severity', at);
    if (entries == null) return const {};
    final pointer = at.child('severity');
    final result = <DiagnosticCode, Severity?>{};
    final names = <DiagnosticCode, String>{};
    for (final MapEntry(key: name, :value) in entries.entries) {
      final at = pointer.child(name);
      final code = _codes[name];
      if (code == null) {
        diagnostics.report(
          .configUnknownKey,
          'Unknown diagnostic code "$name".',
          file: file,
          pointer: at,
          span: spanAt(at),
          fix:
              'Use a code such as TOA201 or its name such as bool-required; '
              'the codes are in `DiagnosticCode` and the CHANGELOG.',
        );
        continue;
      }
      if (names[code] case final first?) {
        invalid(
          '"$name" names the same code as "$first" (${code.id}); '
          'only the first is used.',
          at,
        );
        continue;
      }
      names[code] = name;
      if (value is! String || !_levels.containsKey(value)) {
        invalid(
          'The level of "$name" must be error, warning, info or off.',
          at,
        );
        continue;
      }
      final level = _levels[value];
      if (DiagnosticCode.configCodes.contains(code)) {
        invalid(
          'The level of ${code.id} ${code.slug} cannot be changed: the '
          'config is read before levels apply.',
          at,
        );
        continue;
      }
      if (DiagnosticCode.fixedCodes.contains(code) &&
          (level == null || level.index > code.defaultSeverity.index)) {
        invalid(
          '${code.id} ${code.slug} cannot be lowered or turned off: '
          'without it a file that cannot be read would pass.',
          at,
        );
        continue;
      }
      result[code] = level;
    }
    return result;
  }

  static final _codes = {
    for (final code in DiagnosticCode.values) ...{
      code.id: code,
      code.slug: code,
    },
  };

  static const _levels = <String, Severity?>{
    'error': .error,
    'warning': .warning,
    'info': .info,
    'off': null,
  };

  /// `preset` of [parent] at [at]; null, reported, for an unknown preset.
  Preset? presetAt(Map<String, Object?> parent, JsonPointer at) {
    final name = stringAt(parent, 'preset', at);
    if (name == null) return null;
    final preset = Preset.values.asNameMap()[name];
    if (preset != null) return preset;
    final known = Preset.values.map((v) => v.name).toList()..sort();
    invalid(
      'Unknown preset "$name". Known presets: ${known.join(', ')}.',
      at.child('preset'),
    );
    return null;
  }

  /// Reports [message] as an invalid value at [at] (TOA902).
  void invalid(String message, JsonPointer at) {
    diagnostics.report(
      .configInvalid,
      message,
      file: file,
      pointer: at,
      span: spanAt(at),
    );
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/custom_types_reader.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import '../overrides/override_set.dart';
import 'config_sources.dart';

/// Reads the custom types of `types.formats` and `types.schemas` (R6.8),
/// entry by entry from every source (`defaults` first, so a client's entry
/// wins). A bad entry is reported through the `invalid` function (TOA902)
/// and left out.
final class CustomTypesReader {
  /// Creates a reader of [_sources] that reports a bad value through
  /// [_invalid] and an unknown key through [_checkKeys].
  const CustomTypesReader(this._sources, this._invalid, this._checkKeys);

  static const _customTypeKeys = {'type', 'import', 'converter'};

  /// The maps to read, `defaults` first, with their pointers.
  final ConfigSources _sources;

  final void Function(String message, JsonPointer at) _invalid;

  final void Function(
    Map<String, Object?> map,
    Set<String> allowed,
    JsonPointer at,
  )
  _checkKeys;

  /// The custom types under `types.<key>`, by format or schema key.
  Map<String, DartTypeOverride> read(String key) {
    final result = <String, DartTypeOverride>{};
    for (final (map, at) in _sources) {
      final types = map['types'];
      if (types is! Map<String, Object?> || !types.containsKey(key)) {
        continue;
      }
      final pointer = at.child('types').child(key);
      final entries = types[key];
      if (entries is! Map<String, Object?>) {
        _invalid('"$key" must be a mapping.', pointer);
        continue;
      }
      for (final MapEntry(key: name, :value) in entries.entries) {
        final custom = _customType(value, pointer.child(name));
        if (custom != null) result[name] = custom;
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  DartTypeOverride? _customType(Object? value, JsonPointer at) {
    if (value is! Map<String, Object?>) {
      return _report('A custom type must be a mapping.', at);
    }
    _checkKeys(value, _customTypeKeys, at);
    final type = value['type'];
    final import = value['import'];
    final converter = value['converter'];
    if (type is! String || type.isEmpty) {
      return _report('A custom type needs "type", a Dart type name.', at);
    }
    if (import is! String || import.isEmpty) {
      return _report(
        'A custom type needs "import", the library that declares "$type".',
        at,
      );
    }
    if (converter != null && (converter is! String || converter.isEmpty)) {
      return _report(
        '"converter" must be a non-empty string.',
        at.child('converter'),
      );
    }
    if (!_checkFields(type, import, converter as String?, at)) return null;
    return DartTypeOverride(type: type, import: import, converter: converter);
  }

  /// Whether every field of a custom type is valid; reports each that is
  /// not.
  bool _checkFields(
    String type,
    String import,
    String? converter,
    JsonPointer at,
  ) {
    var valid = true;
    for (final (field, written) in [
      ('type', type),
      ('import', import),
      if (converter != null) ('converter', converter),
    ]) {
      if (DartTypeOverride.problem(field, written) case final problem?) {
        _invalid(problem, at.child(field));
        valid = false;
      }
    }
    return valid;
  }

  /// Reports [message] at [at]; returns null.
  Null _report(String message, JsonPointer at) {
    _invalid(message, at);
    return null;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/generation_reader.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import '../ir/names.dart';
import '../overrides/override_set.dart';
import 'config_keys.dart';
import 'config_reader.dart';
import 'config_sources.dart';
import 'custom_types_reader.dart';
import 'generation_config.dart';
import 'operation_filter_reader.dart';
import 'operation_settings_reader.dart';
import 'type_settings_reader.dart';

/// Reads the generation settings of a client: each key from the client, or
/// from `defaults` when the client does not set it; `naming`, `types` and
/// `errors` key by key, and `types.formats` and `types.schemas` entry by
/// entry.
final class GenerationReader {
  /// Creates a reader of [sources], `defaults` first, that reports through
  /// [_reader].
  GenerationReader(
    this._reader,
    List<(Map<String, Object?>, JsonPointer)> sources,
  ) : _sources = ConfigSources(sources);

  static final _identifier = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*$');

  final ConfigReader _reader;

  /// The maps to read, `defaults` first.
  final ConfigSources _sources;

  /// The settings, with the defaults where a value is missing or bad.
  GenerationConfig read() {
    _checkSectionKeys();
    final suffixes = _suffixes();
    final client = _sources.last.$2;
    final errorSchema = _string('errors', 'default_schema');
    final operations = OperationSettingsReader(_sources, _reader.invalid);
    return GenerationConfig(
      groupTemplate: _groupTemplate() ?? GenerationConfig.defaultGroupTemplate,
      defaultGroup: _defaultGroup() ?? GenerationConfig.defaultDefaultGroup,
      inputSuffix: suffixes.$1,
      patchSuffix: suffixes.$2,
      operationPrefix: operations.operationPrefix(),
      streamSuffix: operations.streamSuffix(),
      streamReconnect: operations.streamReconnect(),
      clientClass: _clientClass(),
      groupBy: _choice('group_by', GroupBy.values) ?? .tag,
      include: _filters.read('include'),
      exclude: _filters.read('exclude'),
      numberType: TypeSettingsReader(_sources, _reader.invalid).numberType(),
      formats: _customTypes.read('formats'),
      schemas: _customTypes.read('schemas'),
      triState: _choice('tri_state', TriStateRule.values) ?? .patch,
      union: _choice('union', UnionMode.values) ?? .raw,
      defaultErrorSchema: errorSchema?.$1,
      defaultErrorSchemaPointer: errorSchema?.$2,
      defaultErrorSchemaSpan: errorSchema == null
          ? null
          : _reader.spanAt(errorSchema.$2),
      file: _reader.file,
      client: client,
      clientSpan: _reader.spanAt(client),
    );
  }

  CustomTypesReader get _customTypes =>
      CustomTypesReader(_sources, _reader.invalid, _reader.checkKeys);

  OperationFilterReader get _filters => OperationFilterReader(
    _sources,
    _reader.invalid,
    _reader.checkKeys,
    _reader.spanAt,
  );

  void _checkSectionKeys() {
    for (final (map, at) in _sources) {
      for (final (section, keys) in [
        ('naming', ConfigKeys.naming),
        ('types', ConfigKeys.types),
        ('errors', const {'default_schema'}),
        ('sse', const {'reconnect'}),
      ]) {
        if (_reader.mapAt(map, section, at) case final inner?) {
          _reader.checkKeys(inner, keys, at.child(section));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  (String, JsonPointer)? _string(String section, String key) {
    final picked = _sources.pick(key, section);
    if (picked == null) return null;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is String && value.isNotEmpty) return (value, at);
    return _invalid('"$key" must be a non-empty string.', at);
  }

  String? _groupTemplate() {
    final picked = _string('naming', 'group');
    if (picked == null) return null;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    final probe = value.replaceAll('{Tag}', 'Tag');
    if (value.contains('{Tag}') && _identifier.hasMatch(probe)) return value;
    return _invalid(
      '"group" must contain {Tag} and give a Dart identifier, such as '
      '{Tag}Api.',
      at,
    );
  }

  String? _identifierAt(String key) {
    final picked = _sources.pick(key, 'naming');
    if (picked == null) return null;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is String && _identifier.hasMatch(value)) return value;
    return _invalid('"$key" must be a Dart identifier.', at);
  }

  /// `default_group` is the name of a class, so a reserved word or a
  /// built-in identifier cannot be it.
  String? _defaultGroup() {
    final name = _identifierAt('default_group');
    if (name == null) return null;
    if (!DartNames.reservedWords.contains(name) &&
        !DartNames.builtInIdentifiers.contains(name)) {
      return name;
    }
    return _invalid(
      '"default_group" is the name of a class and "$name" is a reserved word '
      'in Dart.',
      _sources.pick('default_group', 'naming')!.$2,
    );
  }

  /// `client` is the name of a class, as `default_group` is.
  String? _clientClass() {
    final name = _identifierAt('client');
    if (name == null) return null;
    if (!DartNames.reservedWords.contains(name) &&
        !DartNames.builtInIdentifiers.contains(name) &&
        name.startsWith(RegExp(r'[A-Z$]'))) {
      return name;
    }
    return _invalid(
      '"client" is the name of a class: an identifier that starts with an '
      'uppercase letter and is not a reserved word in Dart.',
      _sources.pick('client', 'naming')!.$2,
    );
  }

  /// `input_suffix` and `patch_suffix`: two model names made from one schema
  /// must differ, so equal suffixes are an error, reported at the later
  /// key, and both go back to their defaults.
  (String, String) _suffixes() {
    final input = _identifierAt('input_suffix');
    final patch = _identifierAt('patch_suffix');
    final inputSuffix = input ?? GenerationConfig.defaultInputSuffix;
    final patchSuffix = patch ?? GenerationConfig.defaultPatchSuffix;
    if (inputSuffix != patchSuffix) return (inputSuffix, patchSuffix);
    final at = patch != null
        ? _sources.pick('patch_suffix', 'naming')!.$2
        : _sources.pick('input_suffix', 'naming')!.$2;
    _invalid(
      '"input_suffix" and "patch_suffix" must be different, both are '
      '"$inputSuffix"; the defaults ${GenerationConfig.defaultInputSuffix} '
      'and ${GenerationConfig.defaultPatchSuffix} are used.',
      at,
    );
    return (
      GenerationConfig.defaultInputSuffix,
      GenerationConfig.defaultPatchSuffix,
    );
  }

  T? _choice<T extends Enum>(String key, List<T> values) {
    final picked = _sources.pick(key);
    if (picked == null) return null;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    for (final choice in values) {
      if (value == choice.name) return choice;
    }
    return _invalid(
      '"$key" must be one of ${values.map((v) => v.name).join(', ')}.',
      at,
    );
  }

  /// Reports an invalid value at [at]; returns null.
  Null _invalid(String message, JsonPointer at) {
    _reader.invalid(message, at);
    return null;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/operation_filter_reader.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:source_span/source_span.dart';

import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import 'config_sources.dart';
import 'generation_config.dart';

/// Reads the operation filters `include` and `exclude` (R5.4) from the last
/// source that has them. A bad value is reported through the `invalid`
/// function (TOA902) and left out.
final class OperationFilterReader {
  /// Creates a reader of [_sources] that reports a bad value through
  /// [_invalid] and an unknown key through [_checkKeys]; [_spanAt] gives a
  /// pattern its place in the config file.
  const OperationFilterReader(
    this._sources,
    this._invalid,
    this._checkKeys,
    this._spanAt,
  );

  static const _filterKeys = {'tags', 'operations', 'paths'};

  /// The maps to read, `defaults` first, with their pointers.
  final ConfigSources _sources;

  final void Function(String message, JsonPointer at) _invalid;

  final void Function(
    Map<String, Object?> map,
    Set<String> allowed,
    JsonPointer at,
  )
  _checkKeys;

  final SourceSpan? Function(JsonPointer at) _spanAt;

  /// The filter under [key]: `include` or `exclude`.
  OperationFilter read(String key) {
    final picked = _sources.pick(key);
    if (picked == null) return const OperationFilter();
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is! Map<String, Object?>) {
      _invalid('"$key" must be a mapping.', at);
      return const OperationFilter();
    }
    _checkKeys(value, _filterKeys, at);
    return OperationFilter(
      tags: _patterns(value, 'tags', at),
      operations: _patterns(value, 'operations', at),
      paths: _patterns(value, 'paths', at),
    );
  }

  List<FilterPattern> _patterns(
    Map<String, Object?> filter,
    String name,
    JsonPointer at,
  ) {
    final list = filter[name];
    if (list == null) return const [];
    if (list is! List<Object?>) {
      _invalid('"$name" must be a list of strings.', at.child(name));
      return const [];
    }
    final result = <FilterPattern>[];
    for (final (i, item) in list.indexed) {
      final pointer = at.child(name).child(i);
      if (item is String && item.isNotEmpty) {
        result.add(FilterPattern(item, pointer, _spanAt(pointer)));
      } else {
        _invalid('Each entry must be a non-empty string.', pointer);
      }
    }
    return result;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/type_settings_reader.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import 'config_sources.dart';

/// Reads the settings of how a client's types are generated: `types.number`
/// from the last source that has it (`defaults` first). A bad value is
/// reported through the `invalid` function (TOA902) and the default is
/// used.
final class TypeSettingsReader {
  /// Creates a reader of [_sources] that reports through [_invalid].
  const TypeSettingsReader(this._sources, this._invalid);

  /// The maps to read, `defaults` first, with their pointers.
  final ConfigSources _sources;

  final void Function(String message, JsonPointer at) _invalid;

  /// `types.number`: the Dart type of a `number` without a format, `double`
  /// or `int`; null for `num`, the default.
  String? numberType() {
    final picked = _sources.pick('number', 'types');
    if (picked == null) return null;
    final (value, at) = picked;
    if (value is! String || value.isEmpty) {
      return _report('"number" must be a non-empty string.', at);
    }
    return switch (value) {
      'num' => null,
      'double' || 'int' => value,
      _ => _report('"number" must be num, double or int.', at),
    };
  }

  /// Reports [message] at [at]; returns null.
  Null _report(String message, JsonPointer at) {
    _invalid(message, at);
    return null;
  }
}
````

````dart
import 'operation_ir.dart';

/// An API class: the operations of a tag, a path segment or the whole spec
/// (R5.3).
final class GroupIr {
  /// Creates a group; [operations] are sorted by name.
  GroupIr(this.name, List<OperationIr> operations, {this.field = 'api'})
    : operations = List.unmodifiable(
        operations.toList()..sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name)),
      );

  /// The Dart name of the class.
  final String name;

  /// The name of the group's field in the root client (`pets` for
  /// `PetsApi`).
  final String field;

  /// The operations, by method name.
  final List<OperationIr> operations;
}
````

````dart
import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'spec_operation.dart';

/// The group an operation is in before the class name template; [untagged]
/// when it has no group of its own (no tag), so it is in the default group.
final class GroupChoice {
  /// The group [name]; `''` is the default group.
  const GroupChoice(this.name, {this.untagged = false});

  /// The group's word: a tag or a path segment in UpperCamelCase, or an
  /// override's group; `''` for the default group.
  final String name;

  /// Whether the operation is in the default group for want of a tag.
  final bool untagged;
}

/// Puts operations into groups (R5.3) and names the group classes in the
/// library scope.
final class GroupNames {
  /// Creates the namer for the client of [_context].
  const GroupNames(this._context);

  static const _names = DartNames();

  final IrContext _context;

  /// The group of [entry] before the class name template: an override's
  /// `group`, else by `group_by`; [tagOfGroup] remembers the tag or path
  /// segment each group was made from.
  GroupChoice choose(SpecOperation entry, Map<String, String> tagOfGroup) {
    if (_overridden(entry) case final name?) return GroupChoice(name);
    return switch (_context.config.groupBy) {
      .single => const GroupChoice(''),
      .path => _byPath(entry, tagOfGroup),
      .tag => _byTag(entry, tagOfGroup),
    };
  }

  /// The group an override names for [entry], if it names a valid one.
  String? _overridden(SpecOperation entry) {
    final override = _context.overrides.operationAt(entry.operation.location);
    final written = override?.group;
    if (override == null || written == null) return null;
    // A group is a word list like a tag: a value that differs from its
    // UpperCamelCase form only by case or separators is that form.
    final words = _names.pascal(written);
    if (_names.isValid(written, .type)) return written;
    if (words != null && !_names.dropsLetters(written)) return words;
    return _context.checkedOverrideName(
      override,
      'group',
      written,
      .type,
      what: 'group',
    );
  }

  GroupChoice _byPath(SpecOperation entry, Map<String, String> tagOfGroup) {
    final segment = entry.path
        .split('/')
        .firstWhere(
          (s) => s.isNotEmpty && !s.startsWith('{'),
          orElse: () => '',
        );
    final group = _names.pascal(segment) ?? '';
    if (group.isNotEmpty && !tagOfGroup.containsKey(group)) {
      tagOfGroup[group] = segment;
      _context.checkLossyName(
        segment,
        group,
        entry.operation.location,
        what: 'Path segment',
        override: _context.overrideAt(
          ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
          {'group': group},
        ),
      );
    }
    return GroupChoice(group);
  }

  GroupChoice _byTag(SpecOperation entry, Map<String, String> tagOfGroup) {
    final operation = entry.operation;
    if (operation.tags.isEmpty) return const GroupChoice('', untagged: true);
    final tag = operation.tags.first;
    final tagAt = operation.location.child('tags').child(0);
    if (operation.tags.length > 1) _reportTags(entry, tag);
    final group = _names.pascal(tag);
    if (group == null) {
      _context.report(
        .nameUnrepresentable,
        'Tag "$tag" has no ASCII letter or digit, so the operation is in '
        '${_context.config.defaultGroup}.',
        tagAt,
        fix: 'Name the group of the operation.',
        override: _context.overrideAt(
          ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
          {'group': 'MyGroup'},
        ),
      );
      return const GroupChoice('');
    }
    _remember(entry, tag, group, tagOfGroup);
    return GroupChoice(group);
  }

  /// Reports that [entry] has several tags and is in the group of [tag].
  void _reportTags(SpecOperation entry, String tag) {
    final operation = entry.operation;
    _context.report(
      .operationMultipleTags,
      'This operation has tags ${operation.tags.join(', ')}; it is '
      'generated once, in the group of "$tag".',
      operation.location.child('tags'),
      fix: 'Keep one tag at the source, or name the group.',
      override: _context.overrideAt(
        ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
        {'group': _names.pascal(tag) ?? 'MyGroup'},
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Remembers that [tag] made [group], or reports that another tag made it
  /// already (TOA555).
  void _remember(
    SpecOperation entry,
    String tag,
    String group,
    Map<String, String> tagOfGroup,
  ) {
    final tagAt = entry.operation.location.child('tags').child(0);
    final earlier = tagOfGroup[group];
    if (earlier == null) {
      tagOfGroup[group] = tag;
      _context.checkLossyName(
        tag,
        group,
        tagAt,
        what: 'Tag',
        override: _context.overrideAt(
          ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
          {'group': group},
        ),
      );
    } else if (earlier != tag) {
      _context.report(
        .tagsMerged,
        'Tags "$earlier" and "$tag" give the same group name, so their '
        'operations are in one class.',
        tagAt,
        fix: 'Write the tag the same way everywhere at the source.',
      );
    }
  }

  /// The class name of [group] in the library scope [types]; [groupClasses]
  /// are the class names the groups got already.
  String className(
    NameScope types,
    String group,
    SpecLocation site,
    Set<String> groupClasses,
  ) {
    final config = _context.config;
    final candidate = group.isEmpty
        ? config.defaultGroup
        : config.groupTemplate.replaceAll('{Tag}', group);
    final (name, change) = types.claim(candidate);
    groupClasses.add(name);
    if (change != null) {
      final ofGroup = change == .collision && groupClasses.contains(candidate);
      _context.report(
        change == .reserved ? .nameReserved : .nameCollision,
        change == .reserved
            ? 'Group class "$candidate" would be a name Dart already has; it '
                  'is named "$name".'
            : ofGroup
            ? 'The group class "$candidate" is the class of another group '
                  'already; this group\'s class is named "$name".'
            : 'A type is named "$candidate" already; the group class is '
                  'named "$name".',
        site,
        fix: ofGroup
            ? 'Name the group with the group override of its operations, or '
                  'change naming.group or naming.default_group in the config.'
            : 'Change naming.group in the config, or rename the schema.',
      );
    }
    return name;
  }
}
````

**Replace** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir_builder.dart` (the whole file):

````dart
import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import '../model/operation.dart';
import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import '../normalize/normalizer.dart';
import 'api_names.dart';
import 'direction_index.dart';
import 'group_names.dart';
import 'ir.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'method_names.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'operation_builder.dart';
import 'operation_checks.dart';
import 'operation_selector.dart';
import 'schema_mapper.dart';

/// Builds the semantic IR of a client from its normalized spec (M6).
///
/// Operations are grouped (R5.3) and named (R5.1); their parameters,
/// bodies and responses are mapped to types, which creates the
/// declarations they reach (R4.2); then declarations and groups are named
/// in one library scope (R5.2). Problems go to the [DiagnosticBag] given to
/// the constructor; nothing is thrown for any spec.
final class IrBuilder {
  /// Creates a builder that reports to [_diagnostics].
  const IrBuilder(this._diagnostics);

  final DiagnosticBag _diagnostics;

  /// The IR of [spec] generated as [client] says.
  ClientIr build(NormalizedSpec spec, ClientConfig client) {
    final context = IrContext(_diagnostics, spec, client);
    // Reads the setting once, so a key that is not in the spec is reported
    // even when no operation is generated.
    final defaultError = context.defaultErrorSchema;
    final (all, refs) = _operations(context);
    final selected = OperationSelector(context).select(all);
    final directions = DirectionIndex(
      spec.model,
      isExcluded: context.isPropertyExcluded,
    );
    for (final entry in selected) {
      directions.addOperation(entry.operation, entry.item, entry.method);
    }
    if (defaultError != null) {
      directions.add(
        spec.model.document.components?.schemas[defaultError],
        .response,
      );
    }
    final mapper = SchemaMapper(context, directions);
    final operations = OperationBuilder(context, mapper);
    final groupNames = GroupNames(context);
    final groups = <String, List<SpecOperation>>{};
    final groupSites = <String, SpecOperation>{};
    final tagOfGroup = <String, String>{};
    final untagged = <SpecOperation>[];
    for (final entry in selected) {
      final choice = groupNames.choose(entry, tagOfGroup);
      if (choice.untagged) untagged.add(entry);
      final group = choice.name;
      (groups[group] ??= []).add(entry);
      final site = groupSites[group];
      if (site == null ||
          entry.operation.location.compareTo(site.operation.location) < 0) {
        groupSites[group] = entry;
      }
    }
    _reportUntagged(context, selected, untagged);
    final built = <String, List<OperationIr>>{};
    for (final group in groups.keys.toList()..sort()) {
      final scope = NameScope(
        reserved: {...DartNames.reservedWords, ...DartNames.methodNames},
        reservedSuffix: '_',
      );
      final names = MethodNames(context).of(groups[group]!, scope);
      for (final entry in groups[group]!) {
        final methods = operations.build(entry, names[entry]!, scope);
        if (methods.isNotEmpty) (built[group] ??= []).addAll(methods);
      }
    }
    if (built.isEmpty) {
      _reportEmpty(
        context,
        refs: refs,
        filtered: all.length - selected.length,
        unbound: operations.unbound,
        streams: operations.streams,
      );
    }
    OperationChecks(context).webhooks();
    final types = NameScope(
      reserved: {
        ...DartNames.reservedWords,
        ...DartNames.builtInIdentifiers,
        ...DartNames.takenTypeNames,
      },
      reservedSuffix: 'Model',
    );
    // The user's types are in the generated library's scope too: a
    // declaration named like one gets a suffix (TOA551).
    for (final name in context.customTypeNames) {
      types.reserve(name);
    }
    mapper.nameDeclarations(types);
    final groupClasses = <String>{};
    final groupIrs = {
      for (final group in built.keys.toList()..sort())
        group: GroupIr(
          groupNames.className(
            types,
            group,
            groupSites[group]!.operation.location,
            groupClasses,
          ),
          built[group]!,
        ),
    };
    // The types the emitters add for declarations are named after the
    // groups, so they never rename a group or a declaration; M9's are last.
    final declarations = mapper.declarations(types);
    return ApiNames(context, types).client(declarations, groupIrs);
  }

  /// Reports operations without a group: one TOA554 when no generated
  /// operation has a tag (they all go to the default group, but for those
  /// an override names), else one for each.
  static void _reportUntagged(
    IrContext context,
    List<SpecOperation> selected,
    List<SpecOperation> untagged,
  ) {
    if (untagged.isEmpty) return;
    final defaultGroup = context.config.defaultGroup;
    if (selected.every((e) => e.operation.tags.isEmpty)) {
      final all = untagged.length == selected.length;
      context.report(
        .operationUntagged,
        all
            ? 'No operation of the spec has a tag, so all operations are in '
                  '$defaultGroup.'
            : 'No operation of the spec has a tag, so the operations without '
                  'a group override are in $defaultGroup.',
        SpecLocation(
          context.model.document.location.document,
          JsonPointer.root.child('paths'),
        ),
        fix:
            'Tag the operations at the source, or set group_by to path or '
            'single in the config.',
      );
      return;
    }
    for (final entry in untagged) {
      context.report(
        .operationUntagged,
        'This operation has no tag, so it is in $defaultGroup.',
        entry.operation.location,
        fix: 'Tag the operation at the source, or name its group.',
        override: context.overrideAt(
          ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
          {'group': 'Default'},
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  /// Reports TOA563 for a client without any operation, when the spec has
  /// some: [refs] written as `$ref`, [filtered] left out by the config or
  /// the overrides, [unbound] that cannot be generated (TOA556).
  static void _reportEmpty(
    IrContext context, {
    required int refs,
    required int filtered,
    required int unbound,
    required int streams,
  }) {
    if (refs + filtered + unbound + streams == 0) return;
    final reasons = [
      if (filtered > 0)
        '$filtered ${filtered == 1 ? 'is' : 'are'} left out by the include '
            'and exclude filters or by overrides',
      if (refs > 0)
        '$refs ${refs == 1 ? 'is' : 'are'} written as a \$ref (TOA107)',
      if (unbound > 0)
        '$unbound ${unbound == 1 ? 'has' : 'have'} a path variable without a '
            'parameter (TOA556)',
      if (streams > 0)
        '$streams ${streams == 1 ? 'has' : 'have'} event streams the client '
            'cannot express (TOA654)',
    ];
    final fixes = [
      if (filtered > 0) 'Check include, exclude and the overrides.',
      if (refs > 0) 'Write the operations inline at the source.',
      if (unbound > 0) 'Declare the path parameters at the source.',
      if (streams > 0) 'Offer text/event-stream on every success.',
    ];
    final config = context.config;
    final byFilter =
        filtered > 0 &&
        (!config.include.isEmpty || !config.exclude.isEmpty) &&
        config.client != null;
    final message =
        'No operation of this client is generated: ${reasons.join('; ')}.';
    if (byFilter) {
      context.diagnostics.report(
        .clientEmpty,
        message,
        file: config.file,
        pointer: config.client,
        span: config.clientSpan,
        fix: fixes.join(' '),
      );
      return;
    }
    context.report(
      .clientEmpty,
      message,
      SpecLocation(
        context.model.document.location.document,
        JsonPointer.root.child('paths'),
      ),
      fix: fixes.join(' '),
    );
  }

  /// The operations of `paths`, by location, with `additionalOperations`
  /// (OpenAPI 3.2), and how many operations are written as a `$ref` and so
  /// left out. A path item shared through `$ref` gives its operations to
  /// every path that uses it.
  static (List<SpecOperation>, int) _operations(IrContext context) {
    final result = <SpecOperation>[];
    var refs = 0;
    for (final MapEntry(key: path, :value)
        in context.model.document.paths.entries) {
      final item = context.model.resolve(value);
      if (item == null) continue;
      for (final MapEntry(key: method, value: operation)
          in <MapEntry<String, Operation>>[
            ...item.operations.entries,
            ...item.additionalOperations.entries,
          ]) {
        // An operation written as a `$ref` is empty; TOA107 reports it.
        if (operation.unknownKeys.containsKey(r'$ref')) {
          refs++;
          continue;
        }
        result.add(SpecOperation(operation, method, path, item));
      }
    }
    result.sort((a, b) {
      final byPath = a.path.compareTo(b.path);
      if (byPath != 0) return byPath;
      return a.method.compareTo(b.method);
    });
    return (result, refs);
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/method_names.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/path_template.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'spec_operation.dart';

/// Names the methods of a group class (R5.1): an override's name, else
/// `operationId` without the configured prefix, else a name made from the
/// method and the path, in lowerCamelCase.
final class MethodNames {
  /// Creates the namer for the client of [_context].
  const MethodNames(this._context);

  static const _names = DartNames();

  /// The `operationId` prefix the `nestjs` preset removes:
  /// `UserController_get` → `get`.
  static final nestjsPrefix = RegExp('^[A-Za-z0-9]*Controller_');

  final IrContext _context;

  /// The method names of the operations [entries] of one group, in
  /// [scope]. Names that overrides give are claimed first, so an automatic
  /// name that is the same gets the suffix, not the override's.
  Map<SpecOperation, String> of(
    List<SpecOperation> entries,
    NameScope scope,
  ) {
    final overrides = <SpecOperation, String>{};
    for (final entry in entries) {
      final override = _context.overrides.operationAt(entry.operation.location);
      final name = override?.name;
      if (override == null || name == null) continue;
      final checked = _context.checkedOverrideName(
        override,
        'name',
        name,
        .member,
        what: 'method',
      );
      if (checked != null) overrides[entry] = checked;
    }
    final names = <SpecOperation, String>{};
    for (final entry in [
      for (final e in entries)
        if (overrides.containsKey(e)) e,
      for (final e in entries)
        if (!overrides.containsKey(e)) e,
    ]) {
      names[entry] = _methodName(entry, scope, overrides[entry]);
    }
    return names;
  }

  /// The method name of [entry] in its group's [scope]: [override], else
  /// `operationId` without the configured prefix, else [_pathName].
  String _methodName(SpecOperation entry, NameScope scope, String? override) {
    final operation = entry.operation;
    final prefix =
        _context.config.operationPrefix ??
        (_context.client.preset == .nestjs ? nestjsPrefix : null);
    final id = operation.operationId;
    final source = id == null
        ? _pathName(entry.method, entry.path)
        : prefix == null
        ? id
        : _withoutPrefix(id, prefix);
    final candidate = override ?? _names.camel(source);
    final (name, change) = scope.claim(candidate ?? 'operation');
    final at = ['operations', entry.overrideKey];
    if (candidate == null || change != null) {
      _reportChange(entry, candidate, (name, change));
    }
    if (override == null && candidate != null) {
      _context.checkLossyName(
        id == null ? entry.path : source,
        name,
        operation.location,
        what: id == null ? 'Path' : 'operationId',
        override: _context.overrideAt(at, {'name': name}),
      );
    }
    return name;
  }

  /// Reports that [entry]'s method is named `claimed.$1`, not [candidate]
  /// (null when the `operationId` has no ASCII letter or digit).
  void _reportChange(
    SpecOperation entry,
    String? candidate,
    (String, NameChange?) claimed,
  ) {
    final (name, change) = claimed;
    final id = entry.operation.operationId;
    _context.report(
      candidate == null
          ? .nameUnrepresentable
          : change == .reserved
          ? .nameReserved
          : .nameCollision,
      candidate == null
          ? 'operationId "$id" has no ASCII letter or digit; the method is '
                'named "$name".'
          : change == .reserved
          ? 'Method "$candidate" would be a name Dart already has; it is '
                'named "$name".'
          : 'Another method of the group is named "$candidate"; this one '
                'is named "$name".',
      entry.operation.location,
      fix: 'Name the method explicitly to keep the name stable.',
      override: _context.overrideAt(
        ['operations', entry.overrideKey],
        {'name': name},
      ),
    );
  }

  /// The words of a method name made from [method] and [path]: the method,
  /// the words of every path segment without its variables, then, when the
  /// path has variables, `By` and the variables in path order joined by
  /// `And`: `GET /users/{userId}/posts/{postId}` gives
  /// `getUsersPostsByUserIdAndPostId`, `GET /pets/{id}` gives
  /// `getPetsById`.
  static String _pathName(String method, String path) {
    final variables = [
      for (final match in PathTemplate(path).variables) match[1]!,
    ];
    final literal = path.replaceAll(PathTemplate.variable, ' ');
    if (variables.isEmpty) return '$method $literal';
    return '$method $literal By ${variables.join(' And ')}';
  }

  /// [id] without the match of [prefix] when it starts at index 0 and is
  /// not empty; else [id] itself.
  static String _withoutPrefix(String id, RegExp prefix) {
    final match = prefix.matchAsPrefix(id);
    if (match == null || match.end == 0) return id;
    return id.substring(match.end);
  }
}
````

### Task 2: Config `exhaustive` for enums, unions, status types and event unions (decision 3, R14.2)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/doc/configuration.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/config_keys.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/generation_config.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/generation_reader.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/config/type_settings_reader.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/event_union_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/emit/status_type_emitter.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/api_ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/api_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/enum_mapper.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_union_ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_union_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir_operation_json.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/union_mapper.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/config/generation_config_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/exhaustive_config_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/generated_client_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/ir_json_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 1's `TypeSettingsReader`, `GenerationReader`, `ConfigKeys`; M8 `EnumDecl.exhaustive`/`UnionDecl.exhaustive` and the override `exhaustive` (`IrOverrides`); M9 `StatusTypeIr`; M10 `EventUnionIr`.
- Produces: `GenerationConfig.exhaustive` (`bool`, default `true`; in `key` only when false); `TypeSettingsReader.exhaustive()` (TOA902 for a value that is not a bool, then `true`); `StatusTypeIr(name, cases, {bool exhaustive = true})`; `EventUnionIr.exhaustive` and `EventUnionIr.named(..., {required bool exhaustive})`; `IrOperationJson` writes both.

The key is read like `union` and `types.number`: from the client, else `defaults`. Enums and unions take a schema override's `exhaustive` first, then the config (`EnumMapper`, `UnionMapper`); status types (`ApiNames`) and event unions (`EventUnionNames`) take the config. The emitters write `sealed class` or `abstract interface class` and say in the doc comment whether a `switch` "names every one" or "needs a default case". With the default nothing changes in any generated byte. `generated_client_test.dart` gains a client with `exhaustive: false`, so the analyzer gate checks that such code analyzes clean with `--fatal-infos`.
