**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/emit/exhaustive_config_test.dart`:

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/emit/model_library.dart';

import '../helpers/emit_fixture.dart';
import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  const open = GenerationConfig(exhaustive: false);

  test('exhaustive: false makes enums and unions interfaces', () {
    final files = emitClient('pets.yaml', generation: open);
    expect(
      files['models/status.dart'],
      contains('abstract interface class Status {'),
    );
    expect(
      files['models/pet.dart'],
      contains('abstract interface class Pet {'),
    );
  });

  test('exhaustive: false makes status types interfaces', () {
    final api = emitClient(
      'petstore.yaml',
      generation: open,
    )['api/pets_api.dart']!;
    expect(api, contains('abstract interface class CreatePetResponse {'));
    expect(api, contains('abstract interface class CreatePetError {'));
    expect(api, contains('by status: a `switch` needs a default'));
    expect(api, isNot(contains('sealed class')));
  });

  test('exhaustive: false makes event unions interfaces', () {
    final api = emitClient(
      'chat.yaml',
      generation: open,
    )['api/chat_api.dart']!;
    expect(api, contains('abstract interface class FollowChatEvent {'));
    expect(api, contains('by their event name: a `switch` needs a\n'));
  });

  test('a schema override exhaustive: true wins over the config', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /x:
    get:
      operationId: getX
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                type: object
                properties:
                  color: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Color'}
                  size: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Size'}
components:
  schemas:
    Color: {type: string, enum: [red]}
    Size: {type: string, enum: [small]}
''',
      generation: open,
      overrides: 'schemas:\n  Color: {exhaustive: true}\n',
    );
    final files = const ModelLibrary().files(fixture.build());
    expect(files['models/color.dart'], contains('sealed class Color {'));
    expect(
      files['models/size.dart'],
      contains('abstract interface class Size {'),
    );
  });
}
````

````diff
   missing config) at the config file. `SarifReporter` takes `defaultFile`.
 
 ### Added
+- Config `exhaustive` (M12, R14.2), in `defaults` or a client: with
+  `false` the bases of enums, unions, status types and event unions are
+  `abstract interface` in place of `sealed`, so a new value is not a
+  breaking change for a `switch` with a default case. The default `true`
+  changes nothing; an override `exhaustive` on a schema wins.
 - `ApiLibrary` is exported: the files of a whole client from its IR, as
   `generate` writes them (M11). The programmatic guide now writes a whole
   client with it, and a test checks the files equal those of `generate`.
````

````diff
     default_schema: Problem

@@ -68,6 +69,7 @@ The file is a mapping with two keys, `defaults` and `clients`. `clients` is requ
 | `types` | both | no | Dart types for numbers, formats and schemas, see [Types](#types). |
 | `tri_state` | both | no | `patch` (default), `optional` or `extension`. |

   at #/clients/b/colour
-  fix: Allowed keys here: baseline, errors, exclude, group_by, include, input, line_width, naming, output, overrides, preset, rules, severity, sse, tri_state, types, union.
+  fix: Allowed keys here: baseline, errors, exclude, exhaustive, group_by, include, input, line_width, naming, output, overrides, preset, rules, severity, sse, tri_state, types, union.
 ```
 
 ## Precedence
@@ -90,7 +92,7 @@ A client's key wins over `defaults`. Most keys are replaced as a whole; these ar
 | `naming` | Key by key (`client`, `group`, and so on). |
 | `types.formats`, `types.schemas` | Entry by entry. |
 
-`types.number`, `errors.default_schema`, `sse.reconnect`, `group_by`, `tri_state` and `union` take the value of the last source that has them (the client, else `defaults`). `include` and `exclude` take the whole key from the last source that has it: a client `include:` replaces the `defaults` one, it does not extend it. `preset` and `line_width` come from the client, else `defaults`.
+`types.number`, `errors.default_schema`, `sse.reconnect`, `group_by`, `tri_state`, `union` and `exhaustive` take the value of the last source that has them (the client, else `defaults`). `include` and `exclude` take the whole key from the last source that has it: a client `include:` replaces the `defaults` one, it does not extend it. `preset` and `line_width` come from the client, else `defaults`.
 
 > **Gotcha:** A client that sets `include: {tags: [pets]}` forgets a `defaults.include` completely. Repeat what you still want.
 
@@ -182,6 +184,10 @@ So a converter is a class with a `const` constructor and no required arguments,
 
 `union` decides how a `oneOf` or `anyOf` with no discriminator whose variants share a JSON type is generated: `raw` keeps the value as `JsonValue` (TOA453), `structural` tries each variant in order. An override can set it for one schema.
 
+## Exhaustive
+
+`exhaustive` decides whether the bases of enums, unions, the status types of an operation and event unions are `sealed` (`true`, the default) or `abstract interface` (`false`). A `switch` over a sealed type must name every variant, so a value the API adds later breaks it at compile time; over an interface it needs a default case, and a new value is no breaking change for the code that uses the client (see `typed_openapi diff`). An override `exhaustive` on a schema wins. A value that is not `true` or `false` is TOA902 and `true` is used.
+
 ## Errors
 
 `errors.default_schema` is the key of a `components.schemas` schema used as the body of error statuses that declare none. A key that is not in `components.schemas` is TOA652 and ignored.
````

### Task 3: The API snapshot of a client from its IR (decision 1)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/api_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/group_ir.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir_json.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/declaration_snapshots.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/operation_snapshots.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_builder.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_identities.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_json.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/cli_ir_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/helpers/snapshot_fixture.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/ir_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/snapshot/snapshot_builder_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/snapshot/snapshot_json_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ClientIr` (declarations, groups, root, `nameOf`), `DartType.code`, `DeclId`, `SpecLocation`; task 2's `exhaustive` fields.
- Produces: `GroupIr.key` (the tag or path word, `''` for the default group; also in the IR dump); `enum TypeKind {model, enumeration, union, statusType, eventUnion, parts, group, root}`; `enum MemberKind {field, value, variant, statusCase, event, method, parameter, body, groupField, server}`; `SnapshotMember(String name, MemberKind kind, {String? owner, Map<String, String> facts})`; `SnapshotType(String name, TypeKind kind, {bool exhaustive = true, Map<String, String> facts, Map<String, SnapshotMember> members})`; `ApiSnapshot(Map<String, SnapshotType> types)`; `SnapshotBuilder(Uri root).build(ClientIr) -> ApiSnapshot`; `SnapshotIdentities(ClientIr, Uri root)`; `SnapshotJson` with `version` 1, `encode(Map<String, ApiSnapshot>) -> String` and `decode(String) -> Map<String, ApiSnapshot>` (a `FormatException` names what is wrong).

The snapshot holds what decision 1 lists, keyed as "Decisions taken" 1 and 2 say. Every type of an entry is written as Dart code whose declarations are named by their identity (`SnapshotIdentities.key`), so a renamed model changes only its own entry, not the type facts of every field that uses it (the rename is reported once, at the model). Doc comments are kept as the IR has them, so a changed description is a `docs` change. `SnapshotJson` writes the lock text of decision 4 and reads it back strictly: a text that is not JSON, a version other than 1, or an entry of the wrong shape is a `FormatException`, which task 5 turns into TOA835.

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/snapshot_builder.dart';

import 'ir_fixture.dart';

/// The API snapshot of the spec [text] for a client configured by
/// [generation] and [overrides] (the text of an overrides file).
ApiSnapshot snapshotOf(
  String text, {
  GenerationConfig generation = const GenerationConfig(),
  String? overrides,
}) {
  final fixture = IrFixture(text, generation: generation, overrides: overrides);
  final root = fixture.context.model.document.location.document;
  return SnapshotBuilder(root).build(fixture.build());
}

/// The API snapshot of the fixture spec [name] in `test/emit/fixtures`.
ApiSnapshot snapshotOfFixture(
  String name, {
  GenerationConfig generation = const GenerationConfig(),
}) => snapshotOf(
  File(p.join('test', 'emit', 'fixtures', name)).readAsStringSync(),
  generation: generation,
);
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/snapshot/snapshot_builder_test.dart`:

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/model/spec_node.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/snapshot_identities.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';
import '../helpers/snapshot_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  final petstore = snapshotOfFixture('petstore.yaml');

  test('a declaration is keyed by its spec location', () {
    final pet = petstore.types['#/components/schemas/Pet']!;
    expect((pet.name, pet.kind), ('Pet', TypeKind.model));
    expect(pet.members['status']?.facts, {
      'json': 'status',
      'required': 'false',
      'type': 'Status?',
    });
  });

  test('an enum lists its values by their JSON', () {
    final status = petstore.types['#/components/schemas/Status']!;
    expect(status.kind, TypeKind.enumeration);
    expect(status.exhaustive, isTrue);
    expect(status.members.keys, ['"available"', '"sold"']);
    expect(status.facts['known'], 'StatusKnown');
  });

  test('a method is keyed by its operationId, an argument by its method', () {
    final pets = petstore.types['group:Pets']!;
    expect(pets.name, 'PetsApi');
    expect(
      pets.members['createPet']?.facts['returns'],
      'Result<CreatePetResponse, CreatePetError>',
    );
    final body = pets.members['createPet/body']!;
    expect((body.kind, body.owner), (MemberKind.body, 'createPet'));
    final trace = pets.members['listPets/xTrace']!;
    expect(trace.facts['wire'], 'X-Trace');
    expect(trace.facts['in'], 'header');
  });

  test('a status type is keyed by its method and has a case per status', () {
    final success = petstore.types['createPet#success']!;
    expect(success.kind, TypeKind.statusType);
    expect(success.members.keys, ['200', '201', '204']);
    expect(success.members['204']?.facts['type'], 'none');
  });

  test('the root holds the groups and the servers', () {
    final root = petstore.types['root']!;
    expect(root.members['group:Pets']?.name, 'pets');
    expect(root.members['server:1']?.facts['url'], 'http://localhost:8080');
  });

  test('an event union is keyed by its stream method', () {
    final chat = snapshotOfFixture('chat.yaml');
    final events = chat.types['followChat#stream#events']!;
    expect(events.kind, TypeKind.eventUnion);
    expect(events.name, 'FollowChatEvent');
  });

  test('exhaustive: false is kept for enums and status types', () {
    final open = snapshotOfFixture(
      'petstore.yaml',
      generation: const GenerationConfig(exhaustive: false),
    );
    expect(open.types['#/components/schemas/Status']?.exhaustive, isFalse);
    expect(open.types['createPet#success']?.exhaustive, isFalse);
    expect(open.types['#/components/schemas/Pet']?.exhaustive, isTrue);
  });

  test('a repeated operationId keys the operations by method and path', () {
    final snapshot = snapshotOf('''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /a:
    get: {operationId: list, responses: {'204': {description: ok}}}
  /b:
    get: {operationId: list, responses: {'204': {description: ok}}}
''');
    expect(
      snapshot.types['group:']?.members.keys,
      containsAll(['GET /a', 'GET /b']),
    );
  });

  test('a location in another file is keyed by its relative path', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture('''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths: {}
''');
    final ids = SnapshotIdentities(
      fixture.build(),
      Uri.file('/work/spec/api.yaml'),
    );
    final pointer = JsonPointer.parse('#/Pet');
    expect(
      ids.location(SpecLocation(Uri.file('/work/spec/api.yaml'), pointer)),
      '#/Pet',
    );
    expect(
      ids.location(
        SpecLocation(Uri.file('/work/spec/models/pet.yaml'), pointer),
      ),
      'models/pet.yaml#/Pet',
    );
    expect(
      ids.location(SpecLocation(Uri.file('/work/shared/pet.yaml'), pointer)),
      '../shared/pet.yaml#/Pet',
    );
  });
}
````

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/snapshot_json.dart';

import '../helpers/snapshot_fixture.dart';

void main() {
  const json = SnapshotJson();

  test('the same spec gives the same text', () {
    expect(
      json.encode({'a': snapshotOfFixture('petstore.yaml')}),
      json.encode({'a': snapshotOfFixture('petstore.yaml')}),
    );
  });

  test('the text reads back as it was written', () {
    final text = json.encode({
      'pets': snapshotOfFixture('pets.yaml'),
      'chat': snapshotOfFixture('chat.yaml'),
    });
    expect(json.encode(json.decode(text)), text);
  });

  test('a type is written on its own lines, a member on one line', () {
    final text = json.encode({
      'a': const ApiSnapshot({
        '#/components/schemas/Pet': SnapshotType(
          'Pet',
          .model,
          members: {
            'id': SnapshotMember('id', .field, facts: {'type': 'int'}),
          },
        ),
      }),
    });
    expect(text, '''
{
  "version": 1,
  "clients": {
    "a": {
      "#/components/schemas/Pet": {
        "name": "Pet",
        "kind": "model",
        "members": {
          "id": {"name":"id","kind":"field","facts":{"type":"int"}}
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
''');
  });

  test('a lock without clients is valid', () {
    final text = json.encode({});
    expect(json.decode(text), isEmpty);
  });

  group('a text that is not a lock is a FormatException', () {
    for (final (what, text) in [
      ('not JSON', '{'),
      ('another version', '{"version": 2, "clients": {}}'),
      (
        'a type without a kind',
        '{"version": 1, "clients": {"a": {"t": {"name": "T"}}}}',
      ),
      (
        'an unknown kind',
        '{"version": 1, "clients": {"a": {"t": {"name": "T", "kind": "x"}}}}',
      ),
      (
        'a fact that is not a string',
        '{"version": 1, "clients": {"a": {"t": {"name": "T", "kind": "model", '
            '"facts": {"doc": 1}}}}}',
      ),
    ]) {
      test(what, () {
        expect(() => json.decode(text), throwsFormatException);
      });
    }
  });
}
````

````dart
import 'operation_ir.dart';

/// An API class: the operations of a tag, a path segment or the whole spec
/// (R5.3).
final class GroupIr {
  /// Creates a group; [operations] are sorted by name.
  GroupIr(
    this.name,
    List<OperationIr> operations, {
    this.field = 'api',
    this.key = '',
  }) : operations = List.unmodifiable(
         operations.toList()..sort((a, b) => a.name.compareTo(b.name)),
       );

  /// The Dart name of the class.
  final String name;

  /// The group's word: the tag or the path segment in UpperCamelCase, `''`
  /// for the default group; the group's identity in the API snapshot.
  final String key;

  /// The name of the group's field in the root client (`pets` for
  /// `PetsApi`).
  final String field;

  /// The operations, by method name.
  final List<OperationIr> operations;
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart`:

````dart
/// What a [SnapshotType] is in the generated library.
enum TypeKind {
  /// A model class.
  model,

  /// An enum: a sealed or interface class with a known-values enum.
  enumeration,

  /// A union of variants.
  union,

  /// The sealed type of the successes or errors of an operation.
  statusType,

  /// The sealed type of the events of a stream.
  eventUnion,

  /// The class of the parts of a multipart body.
  parts,

  /// An API class of operations.
  group,

  /// The root client.
  root,
}

/// What a [SnapshotMember] is in its type.
enum MemberKind {
  /// A field of a model or of a parts class.
  field,

  /// A value of an enum.
  value,

  /// A variant of a union.
  variant,

  /// A variant of a status type: one status.
  statusCase,

  /// A variant of an event union: one event name.
  event,

  /// A method of a group.
  method,

  /// A named argument of a method that sends a parameter.
  parameter,

  /// The `body` argument of a method.
  body,

  /// A field of the root client that holds a group.
  groupField,

  /// A server of the root client.
  server,
}

/// A member of a type in an [ApiSnapshot]: what code that uses the client
/// can name, with the facts that decide whether a change breaks it.
final class SnapshotMember {
  /// Creates the member [name] of [kind].
  const SnapshotMember(
    this.name,
    this.kind, {
    this.owner,
    this.facts = const {},
  });

  /// The Dart name.
  final String name;

  /// What the member is.
  final MemberKind kind;

  /// The identity of the member this one belongs to: the method of a
  /// parameter or a body; null for a member of the type itself.
  final String? owner;

  /// The facts by key (`type`, `required`, `json`, `doc`, ...), as text.
  final Map<String, String> facts;
}

/// A type of an [ApiSnapshot].
final class SnapshotType {
  /// Creates the type [name] of [kind].
  const SnapshotType(
    this.name,
    this.kind, {
    this.exhaustive = true,
    this.facts = const {},
    this.members = const {},
  });

  /// The Dart name.
  final String name;

  /// What the type is.
  final TypeKind kind;

  /// Whether a `switch` must name every variant (a `sealed` base); only an
  /// enum, a union, a status type or an event union can be false.
  final bool exhaustive;

  /// The facts of the type itself by key, as text.
  final Map<String, String> facts;

  /// The members by identity.
  final Map<String, SnapshotMember> members;
}

/// The public surface of a generated client (M12, decision 1): its types by
/// a stable identity, a spec location or an operation, never a Dart name.
final class ApiSnapshot {
  /// Creates a snapshot of [types] by identity.
  const ApiSnapshot(this.types);

  /// The types by identity.
  final Map<String, SnapshotType> types;
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/declaration_snapshots.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'api_snapshot.dart';
import 'snapshot_identities.dart';

/// The snapshot entries of the declarations of a client: models, enums and
/// unions, with their fields, values and variants.
final class DeclarationSnapshots {
  /// Creates the entries with [_ids].
  const DeclarationSnapshots(this._ids);

  final SnapshotIdentities _ids;

  /// The type of [declaration].
  SnapshotType of(Declaration declaration) => switch (declaration) {
    ModelDecl() => _model(declaration),
    EnumDecl() => _enum(declaration),
    UnionDecl() => _union(declaration),
  };

  /// The facts every declaration has: its documentation.
  static Map<String, String> _common(Declaration declaration) => {
    'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([declaration.description]),
    if (declaration.deprecated) 'deprecated': 'true',
  };

  SnapshotType _model(ModelDecl model) => SnapshotType(
    model.name,
    .model,
    facts: {
      ..._common(model),
      if (model.additionalProperties case final additional?) ...{
        'additional': _ids.text(additional),
        'additionalName': model.additionalPropertiesName,
      },
    },
    members: {for (final field in model.fields) field.name: _field(field)},
  );

  SnapshotMember _field(FieldIr field) => SnapshotMember(
    field.name,
    .field,
    facts: {
      'type': _ids.text(field.type),
      'required': '${field.required}',
      'json': field.jsonKey,
      if (field.constValue case final value?) 'const': jsonEncode(value),
      'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([field.description]),
      if (field.deprecated) 'deprecated': 'true',
    },
  );

  SnapshotType _enum(EnumDecl decl) => SnapshotType(
    decl.name,
    .enumeration,
    exhaustive: decl.exhaustive,
    facts: {
      ..._common(decl),
      'kind': decl.kind.name,
      'known': decl.knownName,
      'unknown': decl.unknownName,
    },
    members: {
      for (final value in decl.values)
        jsonEncode(value.value): SnapshotMember(
          value.name,
          .value,
          facts: {
            'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([value.description]),
          },
        ),
    },
  );

  SnapshotType _union(UnionDecl decl) => SnapshotType(
    decl.name,
    .union,
    exhaustive: decl.exhaustive,
    facts: {
      ..._common(decl),
      'discriminator': ?decl.discriminator,
      'unknown': decl.unknownName,
    },
    members: {
      for (final variant in decl.variants)
        'variant:${_ids.key(variant.type)}': SnapshotMember(
          variant.name,
          .variant,
          facts: {
            'type': _ids.text(variant.type),
            if (variant.discriminatorValues.isNotEmpty)
              'values': variant.discriminatorValues.join(', '),
          },
        ),
    },
  );
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/operation_snapshots.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'api_snapshot.dart';
import 'snapshot_identities.dart';

/// The snapshot entries of the operations of a client: a group with its
/// methods, their arguments and wire facts, and the types the emitter
/// declares for a method (status types, event unions, parts classes).
final class OperationSnapshots {
  /// Creates the entries with [_ids].
  const OperationSnapshots(this._ids);

  final SnapshotIdentities _ids;

  /// The types of [group] by identity: the group, then the types of its
  /// methods.
  Map<String, SnapshotType> of(GroupIr group) => {
    'group:${group.key}': SnapshotType(
      group.name,
      .group,
      members: {
        for (final operation in group.operations) ..._method(operation),
      },
    ),
    for (final operation in group.operations) ..._typesOf(operation),
  };

  /// The method of [operation], then its parameters and its body.
  Map<String, SnapshotMember> _method(OperationIr operation) {
    final id = _ids.method(operation);
    return {
      id: SnapshotMember(
        operation.name,
        .method,
        facts: {
          'returns': 'Result<${_success(operation)}, ${_error(operation)}>',
          'method': operation.method.toUpperCase(),
          'path': operation.path,
          'accept': ?operation.accept,
          'responses': [
            for (final r in operation.responses)
              if (r.contentType case final media?)
                '${r.status} $media'
              else
                r.status,
          ].join(', '),
          'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([
            operation.summary,
            operation.description,
          ]),
          if (operation.deprecated) 'deprecated': 'true',
        },
      ),
      for (final parameter in operation.parameters)
        '$id/${parameter.name}': _parameter(parameter, id),
      '$id/body': ?_body(operation),
    };
  }

  /// The success type `T` of [operation], as the emitter writes it.
  String _success(OperationIr operation) {
    if (operation.successType case final sealed?) return sealed.name;
    if (operation.events case final events?) {
      final item = switch ((events.union, events.data)) {
        (final union?, _) => union.name,
        (null, final data?) => _ids.text(data),
        (null, null) => 'String',
      };
      return 'Stream<SseItem<$item>>';
    }
    final successes = operation.successes;
    if (successes.isEmpty) return 'JsonValue';
    return switch (successes.first.type) {
      final type? => _ids.text(type),
      null => 'void',
    };
  }

  /// The error type `E` of [operation], as the emitter writes it.
  String _error(OperationIr operation) => switch (operation.errors) {
    NoErrors() => 'Never',
    SingleError(:final type) => _ids.text(type),
    ErrorsByStatus() => operation.errorType?.name ?? 'Never',
  };

  SnapshotMember _parameter(ParameterIr parameter, String method) =>
      SnapshotMember(
        parameter.name,
        .parameter,
        owner: method,
        facts: {
          'type': _ids.text(parameter.type),
          'required': '${parameter.required}',
          'in': parameter.placement.name,
          'wire': parameter.wireName,
          'style': ?parameter.style?.name,
          'explode': ?parameter.explode?.toString(),
          if (parameter.allowReserved) 'allowReserved': 'true',
          'media': ?parameter.contentType,
          'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([parameter.description]),
          if (parameter.deprecated) 'deprecated': 'true',
        },
      );

  /// The `body` argument of [operation]; null when it takes none.
  SnapshotMember? _body(OperationIr operation) {
    final body = operation.body;
    if (body == null || body.kind == .none) return null;
    final type =
        operation.partsName ??
        switch (body.type) {
          _ when body.kind == .bytes => 'FileUpload',
          final type? => _ids.text(type),
          null => null,
        };
    if (type == null) return null;
    return SnapshotMember(
      'body',
      .body,
      owner: _ids.method(operation),
      facts: {
        'type': type,
        'required': '${body.required}',
        'kind': body.kind.name,
        'media': ?body.contentType,
      },
    );
  }

  /// The status types, the event union and the parts class of [operation].
  Map<String, SnapshotType> _typesOf(OperationIr operation) {
    final id = _ids.method(operation);
    return {
      if (operation.successType case final type?)
        '$id#success': _statusType(type),
      if (operation.errorType case final type?) '$id#error': _statusType(type),
      if (operation.events?.union case final union?)
        '$id#events': _eventUnion(union),
      if ((operation.partsName, operation.body?.parts) case (
        final name?,
        final parts?,
      ))
        '$id#parts': _parts(name, parts),
    };
  }

  SnapshotType _statusType(StatusTypeIr type) => SnapshotType(
    type.name,
    .statusType,
    exhaustive: type.exhaustive,
    members: {
      for (final variant in type.cases)
        variant.status: SnapshotMember(
          variant.name,
          .statusCase,
          facts: {
            'type': switch (variant.type) {
              final body? => _ids.text(body),
              null => 'none',
            },
          },
        ),
    },
  );

  SnapshotType _eventUnion(EventUnionIr union) => SnapshotType(
    union.name,
    .eventUnion,
    exhaustive: union.exhaustive,
    facts: {if (union.keepUnknown) 'unknown': union.unknownName},
    members: {
      for (final variant in union.variants)
        variant.event: SnapshotMember(
          variant.name,
          .event,
          facts: {
            'data': switch (variant.data) {
              final data? => _ids.text(data),
              null => variant.hasData ? 'String' : 'none',
            },
          },
        ),
    },
  );

  SnapshotType _parts(String name, List<PartIr> parts) => SnapshotType(
    name,
    .parts,
    members: {
      for (final part in parts)
        part.name: SnapshotMember(
          part.name,
          .field,
          facts: {
            'type': _ids.text(part.type),
            'required': '${part.required}',
            'json': part.wireName,
            'media': ?part.contentType,
            'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([part.description]),
          },
        ),
    },
  );
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_builder.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import 'api_snapshot.dart';
import 'declaration_snapshots.dart';
import 'operation_snapshots.dart';
import 'snapshot_identities.dart';

/// Builds the [ApiSnapshot] of a client from its IR (M12, decision 1): the
/// emitters' only input, so the snapshot is what the generated code
/// declares, without reading that code.
final class SnapshotBuilder {
  /// Creates a builder for a spec whose root document is [_root].
  const SnapshotBuilder(this._root);

  final Uri _root;

  /// The snapshot of [ir], its types sorted by identity.
  ApiSnapshot build(ClientIr ir) {
    final ids = SnapshotIdentities(ir, _root);
    final declarations = DeclarationSnapshots(ids);
    final operations = OperationSnapshots(ids);
    final types = {
      for (final declaration in ir.declarations)
        ids.declaration(declaration.id): declarations.of(declaration),
      for (final group in ir.groups) ...operations.of(group),
      'root': _rootType(ir),
    };
    return ApiSnapshot({
      for (final id in types.keys.toList()..sort()) id: types[id]!,
    });
  }

  /// The root client: a field per group and the servers.
  static SnapshotType _rootType(ClientIr ir) => SnapshotType(
    ir.root.name,
    .root,
    members: {
      for (final group in ir.groups)
        'group:${group.key}': SnapshotMember(
          group.field,
          .groupField,
          facts: {'type': group.name},
        ),
      for (final (index, server) in ir.root.servers.indexed)
        'server:$index': SnapshotMember(
          server.name,
          .server,
          facts: {
            'url': server.url,
            if (server.variables.isNotEmpty)
              'variables': [
                for (final v in server.variables)
                  if (v.defaultValue == null) v.name else '${v.name}?',
              ].join(', '),
            'doc': ?SnapshotIdentities.doc([server.description]),
          },
        ),
    },
  );
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_identities.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../ir/dart_type.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import '../model/spec_node.dart';

/// The stable identities of a client's API (M12, decision 1): what an entry
/// of the snapshot is keyed by, so that it is the same entry in the next
/// run whatever its Dart name.
///
/// A declaration is its spec location, with the file relative to the
/// directory of the root document (empty for the root document itself) and
/// `@input` or `@patch` for the request models of a split schema. An
/// operation is its `operationId` when no other operation of the client
/// has it, else `METHOD /path`; the method that returns its events adds
/// `#stream`.
final class SnapshotIdentities {
  /// Creates the identities of [_ir], whose spec's root document is
  /// [_root].
  SnapshotIdentities(this._ir, this._root)
    : _sharedIds = _repeatedIds(_ir.groups);

  final ClientIr _ir;
  final Uri _root;
  final Set<String> _sharedIds;

  /// The identity of a location in the spec.
  String location(SpecLocation location) {
    final document = location.document;
    final file = document == _root
        ? ''
        : p.url.relative(document.path, from: p.url.dirname(_root.path));
    return '$file${location.pointer.value}';
  }

  /// The identity of the declaration [id].
  String declaration(DeclId id) => switch (id.variant) {
    .base => location(id.location),
    .input => '${location(id.location)}@input',
    .patch => '${location(id.location)}@patch',
  };

  /// The identity of the operation of [operation], without the `#stream`
  /// of its event-stream method.
  String operation(OperationIr operation) {
    final id = operation.operationId;
    return id != null && !_sharedIds.contains(id)
        ? id
        : '${operation.method.toUpperCase()} ${operation.path}';
  }

  /// The identity of the method of [operation].
  String method(OperationIr operation) =>
      '${this.operation(operation)}${operation.events == null ? '' : '#stream'}';

  /// [type] as Dart code: what the generated code declares.
  String text(DartType type) => type.code(_ir.nameOf);

  /// [type] with declarations by identity: equal in two runs when the type
  /// is the same, whatever the declarations are named.
  String key(DartType type) => type.code(declaration);

  /// The fingerprint of documentation [texts]: the first 12 hex digits of
  /// their SHA-256; null without any.
  static String? doc(List<String?> texts) {
    final present = texts.nonNulls.toList();
    if (present.isEmpty) return null;
    final digest = sha256.convert(utf8.encode(jsonEncode(present)));
    return '$digest'.substring(0, 12);
  }

  /// The `operationId`s that more than one operation of [groups] has.
  static Set<String> _repeatedIds(List<GroupIr> groups) {
    final routes = <String, Set<String>>{};
    for (final group in groups) {
      for (final operation in group.operations) {
        if (operation.operationId case final id?) {
          (routes[id] ??= {}).add('${operation.method} ${operation.path}');
        }
      }
    }
    return {
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in routes.entries)
        if (value.length > 1) key,
    };
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_json.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'api_snapshot.dart';

/// Reads and writes the snapshots of clients as the text of
/// `typed_openapi.lock` (M12, decision 6): JSON with sorted keys, a type's
/// lines apart and each member on one line, so a change of the API is a
/// readable change of the file.
final class SnapshotJson {
  /// Creates the codec.
  const SnapshotJson();

  /// The version of the format.
  static const version = 1;

  /// The text of [clients], by client name.
  String encode(Map<String, ApiSnapshot> clients) {
    final out = StringBuffer('{\n  "version": $version,\n  "clients": {');
    final names = clients.keys.toList()..sort();
    for (final (i, name) in names.indexed) {
      out.write('${i == 0 ? '' : ','}\n    ${jsonEncode(name)}: {');
      final types = clients[name]!.types;
      final ids = types.keys.toList()..sort();
      for (final (j, id) in ids.indexed) {
        out
          ..write('${j == 0 ? '' : ','}\n      ${jsonEncode(id)}: ')
          ..write(_type(types[id]!));
      }
      out.write(ids.isEmpty ? '}' : '\n    }');
    }
    out.write(names.isEmpty ? '}\n}\n' : '\n  }\n}\n');
    return '$out';
  }

  String _type(SnapshotType type) {
    final lines = [
      '"name": ${jsonEncode(type.name)}',
      '"kind": ${jsonEncode(type.kind.name)}',
      if (!type.exhaustive) '"exhaustive": false',
      if (type.facts.isNotEmpty) '"facts": ${jsonEncode(_sorted(type.facts))}',
      if (type.members.isNotEmpty) '"members": ${_members(type.members)}',
    ];
    return '{\n        ${lines.join(',\n        ')}\n      }';
  }

  String _members(Map<String, SnapshotMember> members) {
    final ids = members.keys.toList()..sort();
    final lines = [
      for (final id in ids)
        '${jsonEncode(id)}: ${jsonEncode(_member(members[id]!))}',
    ];
    return '{\n          ${lines.join(',\n          ')}\n        }';
  }

  static Map<String, Object?> _member(SnapshotMember member) => {
    'name': member.name,
    'kind': member.kind.name,
    'owner': ?member.owner,
    if (member.facts.isNotEmpty) 'facts': _sorted(member.facts),
  };

  static Map<String, String> _sorted(Map<String, String> facts) => {
    for (final key in facts.keys.toList()..sort()) key: facts[key]!,
  };

  /// The snapshots of [text] by client name.
  ///
  /// Throws a [FormatException] when [text] is not JSON of this format.
  Map<String, ApiSnapshot> decode(String text) {
    final Object? json;
    try {
      json = jsonDecode(text);
    } on FormatException catch (e) {
      throw FormatException('not JSON: ${e.message}');
    }
    if (json case {
      'version': version,
      'clients': final Map<String, Object?> clients,
    }) {
      return {
        for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in clients.entries)
          key: ApiSnapshot(_types(_map(value, key))),
      };
    }
    throw const FormatException(
      'expected an object with "version": $version and "clients"',
    );
  }

  static Map<String, SnapshotType> _types(Map<String, Object?> json) => {
    for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in json.entries)
      key: switch (value) {
        {
              'name': final String name,
              'kind': final String kind,
            } &&
            final Map<String, Object?> type =>
          SnapshotType(
            name,
            _enum(TypeKind.values, kind, key),
            exhaustive: type['exhaustive'] != false,
            facts: _facts(type['facts'], key),
            members: {
              for (final MapEntry(key: id, :value) in _map(
                type['members'],
                key,
              ).entries)
                id: _memberOf(value, '$key $id'),
            },
          ),
        _ => throw FormatException('type "$key" has no name or kind'),
      },
  };

  static SnapshotMember _memberOf(Object? json, String at) => switch (json) {
    {'name': final String name, 'kind': final String kind} &&
        final Map<String, Object?> member =>
      SnapshotMember(
        name,
        _enum(MemberKind.values, kind, at),
        owner: switch (member['owner']) {
          final String owner => owner,
          null => null,
          _ => throw FormatException('member "$at" has an owner not a string'),
        },
        facts: _facts(member['facts'], at),
      ),
    _ => throw FormatException('member "$at" has no name or kind'),
  };

  static Map<String, String> _facts(Object? json, String at) => {
    for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in _map(json, at).entries)
      key: value is String
          ? value
          : throw FormatException('fact "$key" of "$at" is not a string'),
  };

  /// [json] as an object; an absent one is empty.
  static Map<String, Object?> _map(Object? json, String at) => switch (json) {
    null => const {},
    final Map<String, Object?> map => map,
    _ => throw FormatException('"$at" is not an object'),
  };

  static T _enum<T extends Enum>(List<T> values, String name, String at) {
    for (final value in values) {
      if (value.name == name) return value;
    }
    throw FormatException('"$at" has an unknown kind "$name"');
  }
}
````

### Task 4: Five categories of change (decisions 2, 3 and 7)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/api_change.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/api_diff.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/fact_changes.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/member_changes.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/rename_hints.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/diff/api_diff_rules_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/diff/api_diff_spec_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 3's `ApiSnapshot`, `SnapshotType`, `SnapshotMember`, `TypeKind`, `MemberKind`, and `SnapshotFixture` (test helper) for the tests that build snapshots from specs.
- Produces: `enum ChangeCategory {breaking('breaking'), switchBreaking('switch-breaking'), wire('wire'), additive('additive'), docs('docs')}` with `label` and `static ChangeCategory? parse(String)`; `ApiChange(ChangeCategory category, String message, {required String type, String? member, String? fix})`, `Comparable` (category, then type, then member) with `toJson()`; `ApiDiff().compare(ApiSnapshot before, ApiSnapshot after) -> List<ApiChange>`; `FactChanges` with the sets `breaking`, `wire`, `docs`, `categoryOf(fact, before, after)` and `describe(what, fact, before, after)`; `MemberChanges().of(id, before, after)`; `RenameHints(before, after).fixFor(id) -> String?`.

The rule table is "Decisions taken" 3. Each rule has a test in `api_diff_rules_test.dart`; `api_diff_spec_test.dart` runs the classifier on snapshots of real spec changes (a new enum value with `exhaustive` true and false, a new optional query parameter, a new path, a renamed schema) and checks that the table knows every fact the snapshot builder writes. A removed component schema gets the fix of decision 7 when the same file gained exactly one schema of the same kind: `schemas:\n  <NewKey>:\n    name: <OldName>` (a key YAML would misread is quoted); with several candidates the key is the template `<NewKey>`. A fact that appears or goes is worded as such ("accept is now application/json", "const "none" is gone"): the history check of task 8 met Stripe's `setup_future_usage` constant `none`, which "changed from "none" to none" made unreadable.

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/api_change.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/api_diff.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/fact_changes.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';

/// The categories of the changes from [before] to [after], each snapshot
/// one type `t`.
List<ChangeCategory> categories(SnapshotType? before, SnapshotType? after) => [
  for (final change in const ApiDiff().compare(
    ApiSnapshot({'t': ?before}),
    ApiSnapshot({'t': ?after}),
  ))
    change.category,
];

SnapshotType model(Map<String, SnapshotMember> members) =>
    SnapshotType('T', .model, members: members);

SnapshotMember field({String required = 'false', String type = 'int?'}) =>
    SnapshotMember('f', .field, facts: {'required': required, 'type': type});

SnapshotType enumType(List<String> values, {bool exhaustive = true}) =>
    SnapshotType(
      'E',
      .enumeration,
      exhaustive: exhaustive,
      members: {for (final v in values) v: SnapshotMember(v, .value)},
    );

void main() {
  group('types', () {
    test('a removed type is breaking', () {
      expect(categories(model({}), null), [ChangeCategory.breaking]);
    });
    test('an added type is additive', () {
      expect(categories(null, model({})), [ChangeCategory.additive]);
    });
    test('a renamed type is breaking', () {
      expect(categories(model({}), const SnapshotType('U', .model)), [
        ChangeCategory.breaking,
      ]);
    });
    test(
      'a type of another kind is breaking, its members are not compared',
      () {
        expect(
          categories(model({'f': field()}), const SnapshotType('T', .union)),
          [ChangeCategory.breaking],
        );
      },
    );
    test('a sealed type that becomes an interface is switch-breaking', () {
      expect(categories(enumType([]), enumType([], exhaustive: false)), [
        ChangeCategory.switchBreaking,
      ]);
    });
    test('an interface that becomes sealed is breaking', () {
      expect(categories(enumType([], exhaustive: false), enumType([])), [
        ChangeCategory.breaking,
      ]);
    });
  });

  group('members', () {
    test('a removed member is breaking', () {
      expect(categories(model({'f': field()}), model({})), [
        ChangeCategory.breaking,
      ]);
    });
    test('a new optional field is additive', () {
      expect(categories(model({}), model({'f': field()})), [
        ChangeCategory.additive,
      ]);
    });
    test('a new required field is breaking', () {
      expect(categories(model({}), model({'f': field(required: 'true')})), [
        ChangeCategory.breaking,
      ]);
    });
    test('a renamed member is breaking', () {
      expect(
        categories(
          model({'f': field()}),
          model({
            'f': const SnapshotMember(
              'g',
              .field,
              facts: {
                'required': 'false',
                'type': 'int?',
              },
            ),
          }),
        ),
        [ChangeCategory.breaking],
      );
    });
    test('a new value of a sealed enum is switch-breaking', () {
      expect(categories(enumType(['a']), enumType(['a', 'b'])), [
        ChangeCategory.switchBreaking,
      ]);
    });
    test('a new value of a non-exhaustive enum is additive', () {
      expect(
        categories(
          enumType(['a'], exhaustive: false),
          enumType(['a', 'b'], exhaustive: false),
        ),
        [ChangeCategory.additive],
      );
    });
    for (final kind in [
      MemberKind.variant,
      MemberKind.statusCase,
      MemberKind.event,
    ]) {
      test('a new ${kind.name} of a sealed type is switch-breaking', () {
        expect(
          categories(
            const SnapshotType('S', .union),
            SnapshotType(
              'S',
              .union,
              members: {'x': SnapshotMember('X', kind)},
            ),
          ),
          [ChangeCategory.switchBreaking],
        );
      });
    }
    test('a new method is additive, its arguments are part of it', () {
      const group = SnapshotType(
        'G',
        .group,
        members: {
          'op': SnapshotMember('op', .method),
          'op/id': SnapshotMember(
            'id',
            .parameter,
            owner: 'op',
            facts: {
              'required': 'true',
            },
          ),
        },
      );
      expect(categories(const SnapshotType('G', .group), group), [
        ChangeCategory.additive,
      ]);
      expect(categories(group, const SnapshotType('G', .group)), [
        ChangeCategory.breaking,
      ]);
    });
  });

  group('facts', () {
    test('a changed type is breaking', () {
      expect(
        categories(model({'f': field()}), model({'f': field(type: 'String?')})),
        [ChangeCategory.breaking],
      );
    });
    test('an argument that becomes required is breaking', () {
      expect(
        categories(
          model({'f': field()}),
          model({'f': field(required: 'true', type: 'int?')}),
        ),
        [ChangeCategory.breaking],
      );
    });
    test('an argument that becomes optional is additive', () {
      expect(
        categories(
          model({'f': field(required: 'true', type: 'int?')}),
          model({'f': field()}),
        ),
        [ChangeCategory.additive],
      );
    });
    for (final (fact, category) in [
      ('json', ChangeCategory.wire),
      ('path', ChangeCategory.wire),
      ('style', ChangeCategory.wire),
      ('media', ChangeCategory.wire),
      ('responses', ChangeCategory.wire),
      ('doc', ChangeCategory.docs),
      ('deprecated', ChangeCategory.docs),
      ('returns', ChangeCategory.breaking),
      ('an unknown fact', ChangeCategory.breaking),
    ]) {
      test('a changed $fact is ${category.label}', () {
        expect(
          categories(
            SnapshotType('T', .model, facts: {fact: 'a'}),
            SnapshotType('T', .model, facts: {fact: 'b'}),
          ),
          [category],
        );
      });
    }
  });

  group('rename fixes', () {
    const pet = '#/components/schemas/Pet';
    String? fixWhenAdded(List<String> keys) {
      final changes = const ApiDiff().compare(
        ApiSnapshot({pet: model({})}),
        ApiSnapshot({
          for (final key in keys) '#/components/schemas/$key': model({}),
        }),
      );
      return changes.firstWhere((c) => c.type == pet).fix;
    }

    test('one new schema of the kind is named in the fix', () {
      expect(fixWhenAdded(['Animal']), 'schemas:\n  Animal:\n    name: T');
    });
    test('a key YAML would misread is quoted', () {
      expect(
        fixWhenAdded(['a b: c']),
        "schemas:\n  'a b: c':\n    name: T",
      );
    });
    test('several new schemas leave a template', () {
      expect(
        fixWhenAdded(['Animal', 'Beast']),
        'schemas:\n  <NewKey>:\n    name: T',
      );
    });
    test('a type that is not a component schema has no fix', () {
      final changes = const ApiDiff().compare(
        ApiSnapshot({'getPet#success': model({})}),
        const ApiSnapshot({}),
      );
      expect(changes.single.fix, isNull);
    });
  });

  group('wording', () {
    const facts = FactChanges();
    test('a fact that appears says so', () {
      expect(
        facts.describe('Method A.b', 'accept', null, 'application/json'),
        'Method A.b: accept is now application/json.',
      );
    });
    test('a fact that goes says so, also when its value is "none"', () {
      expect(
        facts.describe('Field A.b', 'const', '"none"', null),
        'Field A.b: const "none" is gone.',
      );
    });
  });

  test('changes are sorted by category', () {
    final changes = const ApiDiff().compare(
      ApiSnapshot({
        'a': model({}),
        'b': enumType(['x']),
      }),
      ApiSnapshot({
        'b': enumType(['x', 'y']),
        'c': model({}),
      }),
    );
    expect(
      [for (final c in changes) c.category],
      [
        ChangeCategory.breaking,
        ChangeCategory.switchBreaking,
        ChangeCategory.additive,
      ],
    );
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/diff/api_diff_spec_test.dart`:

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/config/generation_config.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/api_change.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/api_diff.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/fact_changes.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';

import '../helpers/snapshot_fixture.dart';

/// A spec with the schemas [schemas] and the operation `getPet`.
String spec(String schemas, {String parameters = ''}) =>
    '''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets/{id}:
    get:
      operationId: getPet
      parameters:
        - {name: id, in: path, required: true, schema: {type: integer}}
$parameters
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {\$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}
components:
  schemas:
$schemas''';

const pet = r'''
    Pet:
      type: object
      required: [name]
      properties:
        name: {type: string}
        status: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Status'}
    Status: {type: string, enum: [available, sold]}
''';

List<ApiChange> diff(
  String before,
  String after, {
  GenerationConfig generation = const GenerationConfig(),
}) => const ApiDiff().compare(
  snapshotOf(before, generation: generation),
  snapshotOf(after, generation: generation),
);

void main() {
  test('the same spec has no change', () {
    expect(diff(spec(pet), spec(pet)), isEmpty);
  });

  test('a new enum value breaks only an exhaustive switch', () {
    final after = spec(pet.replaceFirst('sold]', 'sold, pending]'));
    final changes = diff(spec(pet), after);
    expect(changes.single.category, ChangeCategory.switchBreaking);
    expect(changes.single.message, 'Enum value Status.pending was added.');
  });

  test('with exhaustive: false a new enum value is additive', () {
    final changes = diff(
      spec(pet),
      spec(pet.replaceFirst('sold]', 'sold, pending]')),
      generation: const GenerationConfig(exhaustive: false),
    );
    expect(changes.single.category, ChangeCategory.additive);
  });

  test('a new optional query parameter is additive', () {
    final changes = diff(
      spec(pet),
      spec(
        pet,
        parameters: '        - {name: q, in: query, schema: {type: string}}',
      ),
    );
    expect(changes.single.category, ChangeCategory.additive);
    expect(changes.single.message, 'Argument DefaultApi.getPet(q) was added.');
  });

  test('a new path is a wire change', () {
    final changes = diff(
      spec(pet),
      spec(pet).replaceFirst('/pets/{id}:', '/animals/{id}:'),
    );
    expect(changes.single.category, ChangeCategory.wire);
    expect(
      changes.single.message,
      'Method DefaultApi.getPet: path changed from /pets/{id} to /animals/{id}.',
    );
  });

  test('a renamed schema is removed and added, with a name override', () {
    final after = spec(
      pet
          .replaceFirst('    Pet:', '    Animal:')
          .replaceAll("schemas/Pet'", "schemas/Animal'"),
    ).replaceAll("schemas/Pet'", "schemas/Animal'");
    final changes = diff(spec(pet), after);
    final removed = changes.firstWhere((c) => c.message.contains('removed'));
    expect(removed.category, ChangeCategory.breaking);
    expect(removed.message, 'Model Pet was removed.');
    expect(removed.fix, 'schemas:\n  Animal:\n    name: Pet');
    expect(
      changes.any(
        (c) =>
            c.category == ChangeCategory.additive &&
            c.message == 'Model Animal was added.',
      ),
      isTrue,
    );
  });

  test('the table knows every fact the snapshot writes', () {
    final facts = <String>{};
    for (final fixture in [
      'petstore.yaml',
      'pets.yaml',
      'everything.yaml',
      'chat.yaml',
      'wire.yaml',
      'hierarchy.yaml',
    ]) {
      for (final type in snapshotOfFixture(fixture).types.values) {
        facts.addAll(type.facts.keys);
        for (final SnapshotMember(facts: f) in type.members.values) {
          facts.addAll(f.keys);
        }
      }
    }
    expect(
      facts.difference({
        ...FactChanges.breaking,
        ...FactChanges.wire,
        ...FactChanges.docs,
        'required',
      }),
      isEmpty,
    );
  });
}
````

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/api_change.dart`:

````dart
/// How a change of the API affects code that uses the client (M12, decision
/// 2), from the most to the least serious: `--fail-on` fails at a category
/// and every category before it.
enum ChangeCategory {
  /// Code that uses the client may no longer compile.
  breaking('breaking'),

  /// Only an exhaustive `switch` over a sealed type may no longer compile.
  switchBreaking('switch-breaking'),

  /// The Dart API is the same, but what goes on the wire changed.
  wire('wire'),

  /// Something was added; code that compiled still compiles.
  additive('additive'),

  /// Only documentation or `@Deprecated` changed.
  docs('docs');

  const ChangeCategory(this.label);

  /// The name in reports and on the command line.
  final String label;

  /// The category named [label]; null for another text.
  static ChangeCategory? parse(String label) {
    for (final category in values) {
      if (category.label == label) return category;
    }
    return null;
  }
}

/// One change between two snapshots of a client's API.
final class ApiChange implements Comparable<ApiChange> {
  /// Creates a change of the type [type], or of its member [member].
  const ApiChange(
    this.category,
    this.message, {
    required this.type,
    this.member,
    this.fix,
  });

  /// How the change affects code that uses the client.
  final ChangeCategory category;

  /// What changed, as a sentence that names the Dart declarations.
  final String message;

  /// The identity of the type that changed.
  final String type;

  /// The identity of the member that changed; null for the type itself.
  final String? member;

  /// A ready override that undoes the change, such as `name:` for a schema
  /// renamed in the spec; null when there is none.
  final String? fix;

  /// Orders by category, type and member.
  @override
  int compareTo(ApiChange other) {
    final byCategory = category.index.compareTo(other.category.index);
    if (byCategory != 0) return byCategory;
    final byType = type.compareTo(other.type);
    if (byType != 0) return byType;
    return (member ?? '').compareTo(other.member ?? '');
  }

  /// The change as plain JSON values.
  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'category': category.label,
    'type': type,
    'member': ?member,
    'message': message,
    'fix': ?fix,
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/api_diff.dart`:

````dart
import '../snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import 'api_change.dart';
import 'fact_changes.dart';
import 'member_changes.dart';
import 'rename_hints.dart';

/// Compares two snapshots of a client's API and sorts every change into a
/// [ChangeCategory] (M12, decision 2).
final class ApiDiff {
  /// Creates the comparer.
  const ApiDiff();

  static const _facts = FactChanges();
  static const _members = MemberChanges();

  /// The changes from [before] to [after], sorted.
  List<ApiChange> compare(ApiSnapshot before, ApiSnapshot after) {
    final old = before.types;
    final now = after.types;
    final hints = RenameHints(before, after);
    return [
      for (final MapEntry(key: id, value: type) in old.entries)
        if (!now.containsKey(id))
          ApiChange(
            .breaking,
            '${_label(type)} ${type.name} was removed.',
            type: id,
            fix: hints.fixFor(id),
          ),
      for (final MapEntry(key: id, value: type) in now.entries)
        if (!old.containsKey(id))
          ApiChange(
            .additive,
            '${_label(type)} ${type.name} was added.',
            type: id,
          ),
      for (final MapEntry(key: id, value: type) in old.entries)
        if (now[id] case final changed?) ..._changed(id, type, changed),
    ]..sort();
  }

  List<ApiChange> _changed(String id, SnapshotType old, SnapshotType now) {
    if (old.kind != now.kind) {
      return [
        ApiChange(
          .breaking,
          '${old.name} changed from a ${_kind(old.kind)} to a '
          '${_kind(now.kind)}.',
          type: id,
        ),
      ];
    }
    return [
      if (old.name != now.name)
        ApiChange(
          .breaking,
          '${_label(old)} ${old.name} was renamed to ${now.name}.',
          type: id,
        ),
      ?_sealing(id, old, now),
      for (final fact in {...old.facts.keys, ...now.facts.keys})
        if (old.facts[fact] != now.facts[fact])
          ApiChange(
            _facts.categoryOf(fact, old.facts[fact], now.facts[fact]),
            _facts.describe(now.name, fact, old.facts[fact], now.facts[fact]),
            type: id,
          ),
      ..._members.of(id, old, now),
    ];
  }

  /// The change of `exhaustive`: a sealed type that becomes an interface
  /// breaks a `switch` without a default, an interface that becomes sealed
  /// breaks a class that implements it.
  static ApiChange? _sealing(String id, SnapshotType old, SnapshotType now) =>
      switch ((old.exhaustive, now.exhaustive)) {
        (true, false) => ApiChange(
          .switchBreaking,
          '${now.name} is no longer sealed: a switch over it needs a default '
          'case.',
          type: id,
        ),
        (false, true) => ApiChange(
          .breaking,
          '${now.name} is now sealed: a class that implements it no longer '
          'compiles.',
          type: id,
        ),
        _ => null,
      };

  static String _label(SnapshotType type) {
    final kind = _kind(type.kind);
    return '${kind[0].toUpperCase()}${kind.substring(1)}';
  }

  static String _kind(TypeKind kind) => switch (kind) {
    .model => 'model',
    .enumeration => 'enum',
    .union => 'union',
    .statusType => 'status type',
    .eventUnion => 'event union',
    .parts => 'parts class',
    .group => 'API class',
    .root => 'root client',
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/fact_changes.dart`:

````dart
import 'api_change.dart';

/// The category of a changed fact of a snapshot entry: the rule table of
/// the facts the snapshot builder writes (M12, decision 2).
final class FactChanges {
  /// Creates the table.
  const FactChanges();

  /// The facts whose change breaks code that names the entry.
  static const breaking = {
    'type',
    'returns',
    'kind',
    'known',
    'unknown',
    'additional',
    'additionalName',
    'data',
    'variables',
  };

  /// The facts that only change what goes on the wire.
  static const wire = {
    'json',
    'const',
    'method',
    'path',
    'accept',
    'responses',
    'in',
    'wire',
    'style',
    'explode',
    'allowReserved',
    'media',
    'discriminator',
    'values',
    'url',
  };

  /// The facts that are documentation.
  static const docs = {'doc', 'deprecated'};

  /// The category of a change of [fact] from [before] to [after] (null
  /// when absent). `required` is breaking when it becomes true and additive
  /// when it becomes false; a fact the table does not know is breaking, so
  /// a new fact is never under-reported.
  ChangeCategory categoryOf(String fact, String? before, String? after) {
    if (fact == 'required') {
      return after == 'true' ? .breaking : .additive;
    }
    if (wire.contains(fact)) return .wire;
    if (docs.contains(fact)) return .docs;
    return .breaking;
  }

  /// A sentence on the change of [fact] of [what]. A fact that appears or
  /// goes is said so, never written as a value: a value may itself be
  /// `none` (Stripe's `setup_future_usage`).
  String describe(String what, String fact, String? before, String? after) =>
      switch (fact) {
        'doc' => '$what: the documentation changed.',
        'deprecated' when after == 'true' => '$what is now deprecated.',
        'deprecated' => '$what is no longer deprecated.',
        'required' when after == 'true' => '$what is now required.',
        'required' => '$what is now optional.',
        _ => switch ((before, after)) {
          (null, final now?) => '$what: $fact is now $now.',
          (final old?, null) => '$what: $fact $old is gone.',
          _ => '$what: $fact changed from $before to $after.',
        },
      };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/member_changes.dart`:

````dart
import '../snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import 'api_change.dart';
import 'fact_changes.dart';

/// The changes of the members of a type that both snapshots have.
final class MemberChanges {
  /// Creates the comparer.
  const MemberChanges();

  static const _facts = FactChanges();

  /// The changes from [before] to [after], the type [id] in both
  /// snapshots.
  List<ApiChange> of(String id, SnapshotType before, SnapshotType after) {
    final old = before.members;
    final now = after.members;
    return [
      for (final key in old.keys)
        if (!now.containsKey(key) && !_ownerGone(old[key]!, now))
          ApiChange(
            .breaking,
            '${_label(old[key]!)} ${_what(before, old[key]!)} was removed.',
            type: id,
            member: key,
          ),
      for (final key in now.keys)
        if (!old.containsKey(key) && !_ownerGone(now[key]!, old))
          ApiChange(
            _added(now[key]!, after),
            '${_label(now[key]!)} ${_what(after, now[key]!)} was added.',
            type: id,
            member: key,
          ),
      for (final key in old.keys)
        if (now[key] case final member?)
          ..._changed(id, key, (before, old[key]!), (after, member)),
    ];
  }

  /// Whether [member] belongs to a member [others] does not have: a
  /// parameter of a method that was added or removed is part of that
  /// change.
  static bool _ownerGone(
    SnapshotMember member,
    Map<String, SnapshotMember> others,
  ) => member.owner != null && !others.containsKey(member.owner);

  /// The category of the new [member] of [type]: a required field or
  /// argument breaks, a new value, variant, case or event breaks an
  /// exhaustive `switch`, anything else is additive.
  static ChangeCategory _added(SnapshotMember member, SnapshotType type) =>
      switch (member.kind) {
        .field ||
        .parameter ||
        .body when member.facts['required'] == 'true' => .breaking,
        .value ||
        .variant ||
        .statusCase ||
        .event when type.exhaustive => .switchBreaking,
        _ => .additive,
      };

  List<ApiChange> _changed(
    String id,
    String key,
    (SnapshotType, SnapshotMember) before,
    (SnapshotType, SnapshotMember) after,
  ) {
    final (oldType, old) = before;
    final (newType, now) = after;
    final what = '${_label(now)} ${_what(newType, now)}';
    return [
      if (old.name != now.name)
        ApiChange(
          .breaking,
          '${_label(old)} ${_what(oldType, old)} was renamed to ${now.name}.',
          type: id,
          member: key,
        ),
      for (final fact in {...old.facts.keys, ...now.facts.keys})
        if (old.facts[fact] != now.facts[fact])
          ApiChange(
            _facts.categoryOf(fact, old.facts[fact], now.facts[fact]),
            _facts.describe(what, fact, old.facts[fact], now.facts[fact]),
            type: id,
            member: key,
          ),
    ];
  }

  /// [member] of [type] as code names it: `Pet.name`, or
  /// `PetsApi.listPets(limit)` for an argument.
  static String _what(SnapshotType type, SnapshotMember member) {
    final owner = type.members[member.owner]?.name;
    return owner == null
        ? '${type.name}.${member.name}'
        : '${type.name}.$owner(${member.name})';
  }

  static String _label(SnapshotMember member) => switch (member.kind) {
    .field => 'Field',
    .value => 'Enum value',
    .variant => 'Union variant',
    .statusCase => 'Status variant',
    .event => 'Event variant',
    .method => 'Method',
    .parameter => 'Argument',
    .body => 'Argument',
    .groupField => 'Field',
    .server => 'Server',
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/rename_hints.dart`:

````dart
import '../snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';

/// The ready `name:` override for a schema that a spec renamed (M12,
/// decision 7): the rename is not guessed, it is reported as removed and
/// added, and the fix keeps the old Dart name on the new schema.
final class RenameHints {
  /// Creates the hints for the change from [_before] to [_after].
  RenameHints(this._before, this._after);

  final ApiSnapshot _before;
  final ApiSnapshot _after;

  static final _component = RegExp(r'^([^#]*)#/components/schemas/([^/@]+)$');
  static final _plainKey = RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*$');

  /// The override that gives the schema that replaced the removed type
  /// [id] its old name: the one component schema of the same kind added in
  /// the same file, else a template with `<NewKey>`; null when [id] is not
  /// a component schema.
  String? fixFor(String id) {
    final match = _component.firstMatch(id);
    final old = _before.types[id];
    if (match == null || old == null) return null;
    final file = match[1]!;
    final added = [
      for (final MapEntry(key: other, value: type) in _after.types.entries)
        if (!_before.types.containsKey(other) && type.kind == old.kind)
          if (_component.firstMatch(other) case final m? when m[1] == file)
            _unescape(m[2]!),
    ];
    final key = added.length == 1 ? _yamlKey(added.single) : '<NewKey>';
    return 'schemas:\n  $key:\n    name: ${old.name}';
  }

  /// A JSON pointer token as text.
  static String _unescape(String token) =>
      token.replaceAll('~1', '/').replaceAll('~0', '~');

  static String _yamlKey(String key) =>
      _plainKey.hasMatch(key) ? key : "'${key.replaceAll("'", "''")}'";
}
````

### Task 5: `typed_openapi.lock` with the API of each client, and `generate --locked` (decisions 4 and 6)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/doc/diagnostics.md`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/api_lock_run.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/client_writer.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/generate_command.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/change_counts.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/api_lock.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/generate_command_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/generate_lock_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 1's `ClientWriter`; task 3's `SnapshotBuilder`, `SnapshotJson`; task 4's `ApiDiff`, `ChangeCategory`.
- Produces: `ChangeCounts(Iterable<ApiChange>)` with `of(ChangeCategory)`, `isEmpty` and `toString()` = `N breaking, N switch-breaking, N wire, N additive, N docs`; `ApiLock(DiagnosticBag, DisplayPaths)` with `static const fileName = 'typed_openapi.lock'`, `static String pathFor(String configPath)`, `Map<String, ApiSnapshot>? read(String path)` (null and TOA835 when the file cannot be used; an empty map when it does not exist) and `void write(String path, Map<String, ApiSnapshot> clients)` (TOA835 on an I/O error); `ApiLockRun.open(bag, paths, configPath, {required bool isLocked}) -> ApiLockRun?` (null when the lock cannot be read: the run writes no client), `static ApiSnapshot snapshotOf(ClientConfig, ClientIr)`, `bool admits(String client, ApiSnapshot)`, `String record(String client, ApiSnapshot)` (the line `API of <client> since typed_openapi.lock: <counts>.`, or `API of <client>: first snapshot in typed_openapi.lock.`), `void finish(Set<String> declared)`; `DiagnosticCode.apiLockDiffers` (`TOA834`, `api-lock-differs`, error) and `apiLockUnusable` (`TOA835`, `api-lock-unusable`, error), both fixed codes; `GenerateCommand` option `--locked`.

`generate` builds the snapshot of each client it writes and prints the line of `record` after the client's own line. At the end `finish` writes the lock once: the new snapshot of each client written, the old one of a declared client that was not (skipped, or not selected), and no section for a client the config no longer declares. With `--locked` the lock is never written: a client whose snapshot differs from its section, or has none, is not written (TOA834 names the counts), and so is a section of a client the config dropped; the line of the client says `Skipped <client>: its API differs from typed_openapi.lock (TOA834).` A lock that cannot be read (merge-conflict markers, a wrong version) is TOA835 and no client is written; the file is left as it was. `doc/cli.md` documents the lock and the categories; `doc/diagnostics.md` the two codes.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/generate_lock_test.dart`:

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/cli/cli.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/snapshot/snapshot_json.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

const _spec = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: listPets
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}
''';

const _pets = '  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/pets}\n';
const _other = '  other: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/other}\n';
const _same =
    'API of pets since typed_openapi.lock: 0 breaking, 0 switch-breaking, '
    '0 wire, 0 additive, 0 docs.';

void main() {
  late TempDir dir;

  setUp(() {
    dir = TempDir();
    dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', _spec);
  });

  Future<(int, String)> generate(
    String clients, {
    List<String> args = const [],
  }) async {
    dir.write('typed_openapi.yaml', 'clients:\n$clients');
    final out = StringBuffer();
    final code = await TypedOpenApiCli(
      out: out,
      err: out,
      workingDirectory: dir.path,
    ).run(['generate', ...args]);
    return (code, out.toString());
  }

  File lockFile() => File(p.join(dir.path, 'typed_openapi.lock'));

  /// Adds the enum value `c` to the spec: a switch-breaking change.
  void addValue() =>
      dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', _spec.replaceFirst('[a, b]', '[a, b, c]'));

  test(
    'the first run writes the snapshot of each client to the lock',
    () async {
      final (code, out) = await generate(_pets);
      expect(code, 0);
      expect(
        out,
        contains('API of pets: first snapshot in typed_openapi.lock.'),
      );
      final lock = const SnapshotJson().decode(lockFile().readAsStringSync());
      expect(lock.keys, ['pets']);
      expect(lock['pets']!.types['#/components/schemas/Pet']!.name, 'Pet');
    },
  );

  test('a run without changes counts none and keeps the lock', () async {
    await generate(_pets);
    final before = lockFile().readAsStringSync();
    final (_, out) = await generate(_pets);
    expect(out, contains(_same));
    expect(lockFile().readAsStringSync(), before);
  });

  test('a run counts the changes by category and records them', () async {
    await generate(_pets);
    addValue();
    final (code, out) = await generate(_pets);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(out, contains('0 breaking, 1 switch-breaking, 0 wire'));
    expect(lockFile().readAsStringSync(), contains(r'"\"c\""'));
  });

  test('a run of one client keeps the snapshot of another', () async {
    await generate(_pets + _other);
    await generate(_pets + _other, args: ['pets']);
    final lock = const SnapshotJson().decode(lockFile().readAsStringSync());
    expect(lock.keys, ['other', 'pets']);
  });

  test('the snapshot of a client the config dropped is removed', () async {
    await generate(_pets + _other);
    await generate(_pets);
    final lock = const SnapshotJson().decode(lockFile().readAsStringSync());
    expect(lock.keys, ['pets']);
  });

  group('--locked', () {
    test('passes when the API is the one in the lock', () async {
      await generate(_pets);
      final before = lockFile().readAsStringSync();
      final (code, out) = await generate(_pets, args: ['--locked']);
      expect(code, 0);
      expect(out, contains(_same));
      expect(lockFile().readAsStringSync(), before);
    });

    test(
      'fails on a changed API and writes neither the client nor the lock',
      () async {
        await generate(_pets);
        final before = lockFile().readAsStringSync();
        addValue();
        final (code, out) = await generate(_pets, args: ['--locked']);
        expect(code, 1);
        expect(out, contains('TOA834'));
        expect(out, contains('0 breaking, 1 switch-breaking'));
        expect(out, contains('Skipped pets: its API differs from'));
        expect(lockFile().readAsStringSync(), before);
        expect(
          File(
            p.join(dir.path, 'gen/pets/models/pet_status.dart'),
          ).readAsStringSync(),
          isNot(contains("'c'")),
        );
      },
    );

    test('fails without a lock and creates none', () async {
      final (code, out) = await generate(_pets, args: ['--locked']);
      expect(code, 1);
      expect(out, contains('has no snapshot of client "pets"'));
      expect(lockFile().existsSync(), isFalse);
    });

    test('fails on the snapshot of a client the config dropped', () async {
      await generate(_pets + _other);
      final (code, out) = await generate(_pets, args: ['--locked']);
      expect(code, 1);
      expect(out, contains('client "other", which the config no longer'));
    });
  });

  test('a lock that is not a lock stops the run and is kept', () async {
    lockFile().writeAsStringSync('<<<<<<< HEAD\n');
    final (code, out) = await generate(_pets);
    expect(code, 1);
    expect(out, contains('TOA835'));
    expect(out, contains('Skipped every client'));
    expect(lockFile().readAsStringSync(), '<<<<<<< HEAD\n');
    expect(Directory(p.join(dir.path, 'gen')).existsSync(), isFalse);
  });
}
````
