diff --git a/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md b/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md
index 9a05872..459995d 100644
--- a/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md
+++ b/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md
@@ -95,16 +95,33 @@ dart run typed_openapi check --format json | jq '.summary'
 | `-c`, `--config` | `typed_openapi.yaml` | Path to the config file. |
 | `--force` | off | Replace files in an output directory that `typed_openapi` did not write or that changed since it wrote them. Each is reported as TOA833. |
 | `--break-lock` | off | Take over a lock when it cannot be told whether the run that holds it still goes. Replaces no file. |
+| `--locked` | off | Check each client against its API snapshot in `typed_openapi.lock` instead of updating the file. A client whose API differs is not written (TOA834). For CI. |
 
 ```text
 $ typed_openapi generate
 Generated shop in lib/src/api/shop: 7 files, 7 written, 0 deleted.
+API of shop: first snapshot in typed_openapi.lock.
 $ typed_openapi generate
 Generated shop in lib/src/api/shop: 7 files, 0 written, 0 deleted.
+API of shop since typed_openapi.lock: 0 breaking, 0 switch-breaking, 0 wire, 0 additive, 0 docs.
 ```
 
 The second run writes nothing, because the output is the same. Ownership, locks and `--force` are explained in [Generated output](generate_output.md).
 
+### The API lock
+
+`generate` keeps the public API of every client it writes in `typed_openapi.lock`, next to `typed_openapi.yaml`: one section per client, with every type, field, enum value, variant, method and argument under a stable identity (the spec location of a schema, the `operationId` or `METHOD /path` of an operation). Commit the file, also when the generated code is git-ignored, as you would `pubspec.lock`. Each run prints, for each client written, how many changes since the lock fall in each category:
+
+| Category | Meaning |
+| --- | --- |
+| `breaking` | Code that uses the client may no longer compile: a type, member or method was removed or renamed, a type changed, or something became required. |
+| `switch-breaking` | A new enum value, union variant, status variant or event appeared; only an exhaustive `switch` over a sealed type breaks. With `exhaustive: false` this is `additive`. |
+| `wire` | The Dart API is the same, but a JSON key, a path, a parameter style, a media type or the statuses changed. |
+| `additive` | A new type, method, or optional field or argument. |
+| `docs` | Only doc comments or `@Deprecated` changed. |
+
+A run of some clients keeps the sections of the others, and drops the section of a client the config no longer declares. `generate --locked` changes no lock: a client whose API differs from its section, or has none, is skipped with TOA834, and so is a section of a client the config no longer declares. A lock that cannot be read, such as one with merge-conflict markers, is TOA835 and the run writes no client: resolve the conflict, or delete the file to start from the current API. This file is not the lock of an output directory (`.<output>.typed_openapi.lock`), which only keeps two runs apart.
+
 ## baseline
 
 A baseline is a file of accepted diagnostics for one client. Both subcommands take `-c`/`--config` and client names, and need a `baseline:` path on the client.
````

--- a/packages/typed_openapi/doc/diagnostics.md
+++ b/packages/typed_openapi/doc/diagnostics.md
@@ -219,6 +219,8 @@ Applying fixes and overrides, baselines, and writing the output.
 | TOA831 | `output-not-written` | error (fixed) | `generate` cannot write a client's output directory, or another run holds its lock (`.<output>.typed_openapi.lock` next to the output; a lock of a process that is gone is taken over, and `--break-lock` takes over one whose owner cannot be told). | Check whether another generate runs on this output. |
 | TOA832 | `output-recovered` | info (fixed) | An earlier `generate` was killed while it replaced the output directory and left the previous tree or a half-built one in sibling directories named `.<output>.typed_openapi-*`. The run put the previous output back, when the output was missing, and removed the leftovers. Also reported when a run takes over the lock of a process that is gone, or breaks a lock whose owner cannot be told with `--break-lock`. | See the message of the diagnostic. |
 | TOA833 | `output-file-replaced` | info (fixed) | `generate --force` overwrote or deleted a file that it did not write or that changed since it wrote it; one report for each file. | See the message of the diagnostic. |
+| TOA834 | `api-lock-differs` | error (fixed) | `generate --locked` found that a client's API differs from its snapshot in `typed_openapi.lock`, or that the lock has no snapshot of it, or one of a client the config no longer declares. The client is not written and the lock is not changed. | Run `generate` without `--locked` and commit the lock; `typed_openapi diff` lists the changes. |
+| TOA835 | `api-lock-unusable` | error (fixed) | `typed_openapi.lock` cannot be read, is not a lock of version 1, or cannot be written. A run that cannot read it writes no client. | Resolve a merge conflict in the file, or delete it to start from the current API. |
 
 ## Config (TOA9xx)
 
@@ -239,7 +241,7 @@ Reading `typed_openapi.yaml`.
 | Mapping to Dart | the other TOA2xx-TOA6xx | the IR builder while it names and types the client |
 | Overrides | TOA560, TOA800-TOA809 | the overrides file, see [Overrides](overrides.md) |
 | Baseline | TOA820-TOA823 | see [Baseline](baseline.md) |
-| Generate | TOA830-TOA833 | see [Generated output](generate_output.md) |
+| Generate | TOA830-TOA835 | see [Generated output](generate_output.md) and [the API lock](cli.md#the-api-lock) |

````dart
import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../diagnostics/json_pointer.dart';
import '../diff/api_diff.dart';
import '../diff/change_counts.dart';
import '../ir/ir.dart';
import '../snapshot/api_lock.dart';
import '../snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import '../snapshot/snapshot_builder.dart';
import '../snapshot/snapshot_json.dart';

/// What one `generate` run does with `typed_openapi.lock` (M12, decisions
/// 4 and 6): it says for each client written how its API changed since the
/// lock and records the new snapshot; with `--locked` it refuses a client
/// whose API differs (TOA834) and changes no lock.
final class ApiLockRun {
  ApiLockRun._(
    this._bag,
    this._paths,
    this._path,
    this._before, {
    required this.isLocked,
  });

  /// The run for the config at [configPath]; null when its lock cannot be
  /// read (TOA835), and then no client may be written.
  static ApiLockRun? open(
    DiagnosticBag bag,
    DisplayPaths paths,
    String configPath, {
    required bool isLocked,
  }) {
    final path = ApiLock.pathFor(paths.absolute(configPath));
    final before = ApiLock(bag, paths).read(path);
    if (before == null) return null;
    return ApiLockRun._(bag, paths, path, before, isLocked: isLocked);
  }

  final DiagnosticBag _bag;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;
  final String _path;
  final Map<String, ApiSnapshot> _before;
  final _written = <String, ApiSnapshot>{};

  /// Whether the run checks against the lock instead of updating it.
  final bool isLocked;

  /// The absolute path of the lock, which no output may hold.
  String get path => _path;

  /// The snapshot of [client] with the IR [ir].
  static ApiSnapshot snapshotOf(ClientConfig client, ClientIr ir) =>
      // The root document's URI as `SpecLoader` makes it, so locations
      // in it are relative to its directory.
      SnapshotBuilder(p.toUri(p.normalize(client.input))).build(ir);

  /// Whether [client] with [snapshot] may be written: always, unless the
  /// run is `--locked` and the lock has no snapshot of it or another one
  /// (TOA834).
  bool admits(String client, ApiSnapshot snapshot) {
    if (!isLocked) return true;
    final locked = _before[client];
    if (locked != null && _same(locked, snapshot)) return true;
    _differs(
      client,
      locked == null
          ? 'has no snapshot of client "$client"'
          : 'differs from the API of client "$client": '
                '${ChangeCounts(const ApiDiff().compare(locked, snapshot))}',
    );
    return false;
  }

  /// Records [snapshot] of the written [client]; returns the line that
  /// counts its changes since the lock.
  String record(String client, ApiSnapshot snapshot) {
    _written[client] = snapshot;
    final locked = _before[client];
    const lock = ApiLock.fileName;
    if (locked == null) return 'API of $client: first snapshot in $lock.';
    final counts = ChangeCounts(const ApiDiff().compare(locked, snapshot));
    return 'API of $client since $lock: $counts.';
  }

  /// Ends the run: writes the lock with the recorded snapshots, keeping
  /// those of the other clients and dropping those of clients the config
  /// no longer declares, [declared]; with `--locked` reports such a
  /// snapshot instead (TOA834) and writes nothing.
  void finish(Set<String> declared) {
    final stale = _before.keys.where((c) => !declared.contains(c)).toList()
      ..sort();
    if (isLocked) {
      for (final client in stale) {
        _differs(
          client,
          'has a snapshot of client "$client", which the '
          'config no longer declares',
        );
      }
      return;
    }
    ApiLock(_bag, _paths).write(_path, {
      for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in _before.entries)
        if (declared.contains(key)) key: value,
      ..._written,
    });
  }

  static bool _same(ApiSnapshot a, ApiSnapshot b) =>
      const SnapshotJson().encode({'': a}) ==
      const SnapshotJson().encode({'': b});

  void _differs(String client, String problem) => _bag.report(
    .apiLockDiffers,
    '${ApiLock.fileName} $problem. Run generate without --locked and commit '
    'the lock; typed_openapi diff lists the changes.',
    file: _paths.of(_path),
    pointer: JsonPointer.root.child('clients').child(client),
  );
}
````

**Replace** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/generate_command.dart` (the whole file):

````dart
import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';
import 'package:io/io.dart' show ExitCode;

import '../check/spec_checker.dart';
import '../config/config_loader.dart';
import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../diagnostics/reporters.dart';
import '../generate/output_writer.dart';
import '../generate/real_paths.dart';
import '../snapshot/api_lock.dart';
import 'api_lock_run.dart';
import 'check_command.dart';
import 'client_writer.dart';

/// `typed_openapi generate`: checks every client as `check` does and
/// writes each client without errors into its output directory: the
/// models, the API classes and the root client (M8, M9).
///
/// A client whose check reports an error, including an overrides file that
/// cannot be used, is skipped; so is one whose output directory the
/// generator may not replace (TOA830): a directory that holds a package, a
/// file the config makes any client read, or a file the generator did not
/// write or that changed since it did (`--force` replaces those and lists
/// each, never the others; `--break-lock` only takes over a lock). The API
/// snapshot of each client written goes into `typed_openapi.lock` next to
/// the config, with a line that counts its changes per category; `--locked`
/// checks the lock instead and skips a client whose API differs (M12). The
/// report and the lines per client go to stdout, and the exit code is the
/// one `check` gives.
final class GenerateCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates the command; reports go to [_out], and files are named as
  /// [_paths] does.
  GenerateCommand(this._out, [this._paths = const DisplayPaths()]) {
    argParser
      ..addOption(
        'config',
        abbr: 'c',
        defaultsTo: 'typed_openapi.yaml',
        help: 'Path to the config file.',
      )
      ..addFlag(
        'force',
        negatable: false,
        help:
            'Replace files in an output directory that typed_openapi did not '
            'write or that changed since it wrote them; each is listed.',
      )
      ..addFlag(
        'break-lock',
        negatable: false,
        help:
            'Take over the lock of an output when it cannot be told whether '
            'the run that holds it is still going. Replaces no file.',
      )
      ..addFlag(
        'locked',
        negatable: false,
        help:
            'Check each client against its API snapshot in '
            'typed_openapi.lock instead of updating it; a client whose API '
            'differs is not written (for CI).',
      );
  }

  final StringSink _out;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  @override
  String get name => 'generate';

  @override
  String get description =>
      'Check specs and generate the clients without errors.';

  @override
  String get invocation =>
      '${runner!.executableName} generate [options] [client...]';

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final diagnostics = DiagnosticBag();
    final configPath = argResults!.option('config')!;
    final config = ConfigLoader(diagnostics, _paths).load(configPath);
    final lines = config == null
        ? const <String>[]
        : _generate(config, configPath, diagnostics);
    _out.writeln(const TextReporter().format(diagnostics));
    lines.forEach(_out.writeln);
    if (config == null) return ExitCode.config.code;
    return diagnostics.hasErrors
        ? CheckCommand.exitHasErrors
        : ExitCode.success.code;
  }

  /// Checks and writes the clients of [config] and updates (or, with
  /// `--locked`, checks) the API lock; returns a line per client.
  List<String> _generate(
    ProjectConfig config,
    String configPath,
    DiagnosticBag diagnostics,
  ) {
    final results = argResults!;
    final only = results.rest.toSet();
    final unknown = only.difference(config.declaredClients);
    if (unknown.isNotEmpty) {
      usageException(
        'Unknown client: ${(unknown.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
      );
    }
    final checks = SpecChecker(diagnostics, _paths).check(config, only: only);
    final lock = ApiLockRun.open(
      diagnostics,
      _paths,
      configPath,
      isLocked: results.flag('locked'),
    );
    if (lock == null) {
      return ['Skipped every client: ${ApiLock.fileName} cannot be used.'];
    }
    final writer = ClientWriter(
      OutputWriter(diagnostics, _paths),
      _paths,
      lock,
      protected: _protected(configPath, config, checks, lock.path),
      overlapping: _overlapping(config.clients),
      force: results.flag('force'),
      breakLock: results.flag('break-lock'),
    );
    final lines = checks.map(writer.write).toList();
    lock.finish(config.declaredClients);
    return lines;
  }

  /// Every file any client of [config] reads, also one that is not run,
  /// and the API lock at [lockPath], by absolute path: an output directory
  /// must not hold one, or the run would delete it.
  List<String> _protected(
    String configPath,
    ProjectConfig config,
    List<ClientCheck> checks,
    String lockPath,
  ) => {
    _paths.absolute(configPath),
    lockPath,
    for (final client in config.clients) ...[
      client.input,
      ?client.overrides,
      ?client.baseline,
    ],
    for (final check in checks) ...check.sources,
  }.toList()..sort();

  /// The names of the clients whose output directory is, or is inside, that
  /// of another client, also through symbolic links or a different case.
  static Set<String> _overlapping(List<ClientConfig> clients) => {
    for (var i = 0; i < clients.length; i++)
      for (var j = i + 1; j < clients.length; j++)
        if (RealPaths.overlap(clients[i].output, clients[j].output)) ...[
          clients[i].name,
          clients[j].name,
        ],
  };
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/change_counts.dart`:

````dart
import 'api_change.dart';

/// The number of changes in each [ChangeCategory]: what `generate` prints
/// for a client and what `diff --fail-on` decides by (M12, decision 4).
final class ChangeCounts {
  /// Counts [changes] by category.
  ChangeCounts(Iterable<ApiChange> changes)
    : _counts = {
        for (final category in ChangeCategory.values)
          category: changes.where((c) => c.category == category).length,
      };

  final Map<ChangeCategory, int> _counts;

  /// The number of changes of [category].
  int of(ChangeCategory category) => _counts[category]!;

  /// Whether there is no change at all.
  bool get isEmpty => _counts.values.every((count) => count == 0);

  /// Every category with its count, in the order of [ChangeCategory]:
  /// `1 breaking, 0 switch-breaking, 0 wire, 2 additive, 0 docs`.
  @override
  String toString() => [
    for (final category in ChangeCategory.values)
      '${of(category)} ${category.label}',
  ].join(', ');
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/api_lock.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import 'api_snapshot.dart';
import 'snapshot_json.dart';

/// Reads and writes `typed_openapi.lock`, the API snapshot of every client
/// next to the config (M12, decision 6). A problem with the file is
/// TOA835.
final class ApiLock {
  /// Creates the reader; problems go to [_bag], files are named as
  /// [_paths] does.
  const ApiLock(this._bag, this._paths);

  final DiagnosticBag _bag;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  /// The name of the lock file.
  static const fileName = 'typed_openapi.lock';

  static const _json = SnapshotJson();

  /// The path of the lock of the config at [configPath].
  static String pathFor(String configPath) =>
      p.join(p.dirname(configPath), fileName);

  /// The snapshots of the lock at [path] by client: empty when there is no
  /// file, null (with TOA835) when it cannot be read or is not a lock.
  Map<String, ApiSnapshot>? read(String path) {
    final file = File(path);
    if (!file.existsSync()) return const {};
    try {
      return _json.decode(file.readAsStringSync());
    } on FileSystemException catch (e) {
      _report(path, 'cannot be read: ${e.message}');
    } on FormatException catch (e) {
      _report(path, 'is not an API lock of version 1: ${e.message}');
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// Writes [clients] to [path] through a temporary file in the same
  /// directory, so an interrupted write never leaves a broken lock, unless
  /// the file holds that text already.
  void write(String path, Map<String, ApiSnapshot> clients) {
    final text = _json.encode(clients);
    final target = File(path);
    final temp = File('$path.tmp');
    try {
      if (target.existsSync() && target.readAsStringSync() == text) return;
      temp.writeAsStringSync(text);
      temp.renameSync(path);
    } on FileSystemException catch (e) {
      try {
        if (temp.existsSync()) temp.deleteSync();
      } on FileSystemException {
        // Nothing more can be done about a leftover temporary file.
      }
      _report(path, 'cannot be written: ${e.message}');
    }
  }

  void _report(String path, String problem) => _bag.report(
    .apiLockUnusable,
    '${_paths.of(path)} $problem. Fix or delete the file and run generate '
    'again.',
    file: _paths.of(path),
  );
}
````

### Task 6: The `diff` command with text, JSON and Markdown reports (decision 4)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/cli.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/diff_command.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/json_diff_report.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/markdown_diff_report.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/text_diff_report.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/diff_command_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/diff/diff_reports_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 5's `ApiLock`, `ApiLockRun.snapshotOf`, `ChangeCounts`; task 4's `ApiDiff`, `ApiChange`, `ChangeCategory.parse`; `SpecChecker.checkClients`; `MessageText.neutralise`.
- Produces: `DiffCommand(StringSink out, StringSink err, [DisplayPaths paths])` registered by `TypedOpenApiCli`; `TextDiffReport`, `JsonDiffReport`, `MarkdownDiffReport`, each `const` with `String format(Map<String, List<ApiChange>> clients)`.

`typed_openapi diff [client...]` checks the clients like `check`, builds the snapshot each would have, compares it with the lock (`--from`, default the lock next to the config) and prints the report; it writes nothing. `--to` compares two lock files and reads no config or spec (it needs `--from`). Without `--fail-on` the exit code is `0` whatever changed; with it, `1` when a change of that category or a more serious one is found (`any` counts every category). A client whose spec cannot be read is named on stderr and the exit code is `1`; an unknown client or `--to` without `--from` is a usage error (64); a lock that cannot be read is TOA835 on stderr and exit `1`. The text report is a line of counts per client, then a line per change and the indented lines of its fix; Markdown has a heading per client and a fenced YAML block per fix, fenced with more backticks than the fix holds, and escapes the characters that would make a link, an image or HTML of spec text; JSON is `{"clients": {"<client>": {"counts": {...}, "changes": [...]}}}`.

````dart
@TestOn('vm')
library;

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/cli/cli.dart';

import '../helpers/temp_dir.dart';

const _spec = r'''
openapi: 3.1.0
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      operationId: listPets
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}
''';

const _config =
    'clients:\n  pets: {input: specs/pets.yaml, output: gen/pets}\n';

void main() {
  late TempDir dir;

  setUp(() {
    dir = TempDir();
    dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', _spec);
    dir.write('typed_openapi.yaml', _config);
  });

  Future<(int, String, String)> run(List<String> args) async {
    final out = StringBuffer();
    final err = StringBuffer();
    final code = await TypedOpenApiCli(
      out: out,
      err: err,
      workingDirectory: dir.path,
    ).run(args);
    return (code, out.toString(), err.toString());
  }

  String lock() =>
      File(p.join(dir.path, 'typed_openapi.lock')).readAsStringSync();

  /// Generates, then changes the spec by [change].
  Future<void> generateThen(String Function(String) change) async {
    await run(['generate']);
    dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', change(_spec));
  }

  String addValue(String spec) => spec.replaceFirst('[a, b]', '[a, b, c]');

  test('reports no change right after generate', () async {
    await run(['generate']);
    final (code, out, _) = await run(['diff']);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(out, 'pets: no changes.\n');
  });

  test('reports a change by category and writes nothing', () async {
    await generateThen(addValue);
    final before = lock();
    final (code, out, _) = await run(['diff']);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(
      out,
      'pets: 0 breaking, 1 switch-breaking, 0 wire, 0 additive, 0 docs.\n'
      '  switch-breaking: Enum value PetStatus.c was added.\n',
    );
    expect(lock(), before);
  });

  for (final (failOn, code) in [
    ('breaking', 0),
    ('switch-breaking', 1),
    ('wire', 1),
    ('any', 1),
  ]) {
    test('--fail-on $failOn exits $code on a switch-breaking change', () async {
      await generateThen(addValue);
      expect((await run(['diff', '--fail-on', failOn])).$1, code);
    });
  }

  test('--fail-on any fails on an additive change, wire does not', () async {
    await generateThen(
      (s) => s.replaceFirst(
        'properties:\n',
        'properties:\n        name: {type: string}\n',
      ),
    );
    expect((await run(['diff', '--fail-on', 'wire'])).$1, 0);
    expect((await run(['diff', '--fail-on', 'any'])).$1, 1);
  });

  test(
    'a renamed schema comes with the override that keeps its name',
    () async {
      await generateThen((s) => s.replaceAll('Pet', 'Animal'));
      final (_, out, _) = await run(['diff']);
      expect(
        out,
        contains(
          '  breaking: Model Pet was removed.\n'
          '    Keep the old name with this override:\n'
          '      schemas:\n'
          '        Animal:\n'
          '          name: Pet\n',
        ),
      );
    },
  );

  test('without a lock every type is new', () async {
    final (code, out, _) = await run(['diff']);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(out, startsWith('pets: 0 breaking, 0 switch-breaking, 0 wire, '));
    expect(out, contains('additive: Model Pet was added.'));
  });

  test('--format json counts and lists the changes', () async {
    await generateThen(addValue);
    final (_, out, _) = await run(['diff', '--format', 'json']);
    final json = jsonDecode(out) as Map<String, Object?>;
    final pets = (json['clients']! as Map<String, Object?>)['pets']!;
    expect((pets as Map<String, Object?>)['counts'], {
      'breaking': 0,
      'switch-breaking': 1,
      'wire': 0,
      'additive': 0,
      'docs': 0,
    });
    expect(pets['changes'], hasLength(1));
  });

  test('--format markdown writes a comment', () async {
    await generateThen(addValue);
    final (_, out, _) = await run(['diff', '--format', 'markdown']);
    expect(out, startsWith('## API changes\n\n### pets: 0 breaking'));
    expect(out, contains('- Enum value PetStatus.c was added.\n'));
  });

  test('--from and --to compare two lock files without a config', () async {
    await run(['generate']);
    File(
      p.join(dir.path, 'typed_openapi.lock'),
    ).copySync(p.join(dir.path, 'old.lock'));
    dir.write('specs/pets.yaml', addValue(_spec));
    await run(['generate']);
    File(p.join(dir.path, 'typed_openapi.yaml')).deleteSync();
    final (code, out, _) = await run([
      'diff',
      '--from',
      'old.lock',
      '--to',
      'typed_openapi.lock',
    ]);
    expect(code, 0);
    expect(out, contains('1 switch-breaking'));
  });

  test('--to without --from is a usage error', () async {
    expect((await run(['diff', '--to', 'typed_openapi.lock'])).$1, 64);
  });

  test('an unknown client is a usage error', () async {
    expect((await run(['diff', 'nope'])).$1, 64);
  });

  test('a lock that cannot be read fails with TOA835 on stderr', () async {
    dir.write('typed_openapi.lock', '{');
    final (code, out, err) = await run(['diff']);
    expect(code, 1);
    expect(out, isEmpty);
    expect(err, contains('TOA835'));
  });
}
````

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/markdown_diff_report.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diff/text_diff_report.dart';

const _removed = ApiChange(
  ChangeCategory.breaking,
  'Model Pet was removed.',
  type: '#/components/schemas/Pet',
  fix: 'schemas:\n  Animal:\n    name: Pet',
);

const _hostile = ApiChange(
  ChangeCategory.wire,
  'Field Pet.a: json changed from "<img src=x>" to "\u001b[2J[x](y)".',
  type: '#/components/schemas/Pet',
  member: 'a',
);

void main() {
  test('text lists each change and the lines of its fix', () {
    expect(
      const TextDiffReport().format({
        'b': [_removed],
        'a': [],
      }),
      'a: no changes.\n'
      'b: 1 breaking, 0 switch-breaking, 0 wire, 0 additive, 0 docs.\n'
      '  breaking: Model Pet was removed.\n'
      '    Keep the old name with this override:\n'
      '      schemas:\n'
      '        Animal:\n'
      '          name: Pet\n',
    );
  });

  test('text neutralises the controls of spec text', () {
    final text = const TextDiffReport().format({
      'a': [_hostile],
    });
    expect(text, contains(r'\u{1b}[2J'));
    expect(text, isNot(contains('\u001b')));
  });

  test('markdown escapes what would mark up the comment', () {
    final text = const MarkdownDiffReport().format({
      'a': [_hostile],
    });
    expect(text, contains(r'"\<img src=x\>"'));
    expect(text, contains(r'\u{1b}\[2J\[x\](y)'));
  });

  test('markdown fences a fix with more backticks than it holds', () {
    final text = const MarkdownDiffReport().format({
      'a': [
        const ApiChange(
          ChangeCategory.breaking,
          'Model X was removed.',
          type: 't',
          fix: "schemas:\n  '````':\n    name: X",
        ),
      ],
    });
    expect(text, contains('  `````yaml\n'));
    expect(text, contains("  '````':\n"));
  });

  test('json counts every category and lists the changes', () {
    final json =
        jsonDecode(
              const JsonDiffReport().format({
                'a': [_removed],
              }),
            )
            as Map<String, Object?>;
    expect(json, {
      'clients': {
        'a': {
          'counts': {
            'breaking': 1,
            'switch-breaking': 0,
            'wire': 0,
            'additive': 0,
            'docs': 0,
          },
          'changes': [_removed.toJson()],
        },
      },
    });
  });
}

+`typed_openapi` has four commands: `check` reads specs and reports problems, `generate` writes the Dart clients, `diff` reports how their API changed, and `baseline` records the problems you accept. +  diff       Report how the API of the clients changed since typed_openapi.lock.
   generate   Check specs and generate the clients without errors.
 ```
 
@@ -122,6 +124,32 @@ The second run writes nothing, because the output is the same. Ownership, locks
 
 A run of some clients keeps the sections of the others, and drops the section of a client the config no longer declares. `generate --locked` changes no lock: a client whose API differs from its section, or has none, is skipped with TOA834, and so is a section of a client the config no longer declares. A lock that cannot be read, such as one with merge-conflict markers, is TOA835 and the run writes no client: resolve the conflict, or delete the file to start from the current API. This file is not the lock of an output directory (`.<output>.typed_openapi.lock`), which only keeps two runs apart.
 
+## diff
+
+`typed_openapi diff [options] [client...]` compares the API of each client in `typed_openapi.lock` with the API the current spec and config give, and lists every change with its category. It writes nothing: run it before `generate` to see what a spec update does to code that uses the client. A client the lock does not have reports every type as `additive`; a client whose spec cannot be read is named on stderr and the exit code is `1`.
+
+| Option | Default | Meaning |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `-c`, `--config` | `typed_openapi.yaml` | Path to the config file. |
+| `--format` | `text` | `text`, `json`, or `markdown` (for a pull request comment). |
+| `--fail-on` | none | Exit `1` when a change of this category or a more serious one is found: `breaking`, `switch-breaking` (also breaking), `wire` (also both breaking kinds), or `any` (every change, `additive` and `docs` included). Without it the command only reports. |
+| `--from` | the lock next to the config | The lock file to compare from, such as the lock of the main branch (`git show main:typed_openapi.lock > base.lock`). |
+| `--to` | the current spec | A second lock file to compare to; with it no config or spec is read. Needs `--from`. |
+
+```text
+$ typed_openapi diff
+shop: 1 breaking, 1 switch-breaking, 0 wire, 1 additive, 0 docs.
+  breaking: Model Pet was removed.
+    Keep the old name with this override:
+      schemas:
+        Animal:
+          name: Pet
+  switch-breaking: Enum value PetStatus.sold was added.
+  additive: Model Animal was added.
+```
+
+A schema renamed in the spec has a new spec location, so it is a new type: `diff` reports the old one as removed and the new one as added, and does not guess that they are the same. When one schema of the same kind was added in the same file, the report gives the override that keeps the old Dart name; otherwise its key is `<NewKey>`. The text and Markdown reports escape control characters from the spec, and the Markdown report also escapes the characters that would mark up the comment.
+
 ## baseline
 
 A baseline is a file of accepted diagnostics for one client. Both subcommands take `-c`/`--config` and client names, and need a `baseline:` path on the client.
@@ -147,7 +175,7 @@ Without a `baseline:` key on any selected client, `baseline create` prints `No c
 | Code | Meaning |
 | --- | --- |
 | `0` | No errors. Warnings and infos do not change it. |
-| `1` | At least one error that a baseline does not accept (also: a client was skipped by `generate`, or a spec could not be read by `baseline`). |
+| `1` | At least one error that a baseline does not accept (also: a client was skipped by `generate`, a spec could not be read by `baseline` or `diff`, or `diff --fail-on` found a change of that category). |
 | `64` | Usage error: unknown client, unknown option, missing subcommand, or no client with a baseline. |
 | `78` | The config file could not be loaded (not found, or not readable as a config at all). |

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/diff_command.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:args/command_runner.dart';
import 'package:io/io.dart' show ExitCode;

import '../check/spec_checker.dart';
import '../config/config_loader.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../diagnostics/reporters.dart';
import '../diff/api_change.dart';
import '../diff/api_diff.dart';
import '../diff/json_diff_report.dart';
import '../diff/markdown_diff_report.dart';
import '../diff/text_diff_report.dart';
import '../snapshot/api_lock.dart';
import '../snapshot/api_snapshot.dart';
import 'api_lock_run.dart';
import 'check_command.dart';

/// `typed_openapi diff`: compares the API of each client in
/// `typed_openapi.lock` with the API the current spec and config give, or
/// two lock files with each other, and reports every change by category
/// (M12, decision 4). It writes nothing.
///
/// The report goes to stdout, the errors of the run to stderr. The exit
/// code is `0` unless `--fail-on` names a category that a change reaches,
/// or a spec or lock cannot be read; then it is `1`.
final class DiffCommand extends Command<int> {
  /// Creates the command; the report goes to [_out], errors to [_err], and
  /// files are named as [_paths] does.
  DiffCommand(this._out, this._err, [this._paths = const DisplayPaths()]) {
    argParser
      ..addOption(
        'config',
        abbr: 'c',
        defaultsTo: 'typed_openapi.yaml',
        help: 'Path to the config file.',
      )
      ..addOption(
        'format',
        allowed: ['text', 'json', 'markdown'],
        defaultsTo: 'text',
        help: 'Report format; markdown is for a pull request comment.',
      )
      ..addOption(
        'fail-on',
        allowed: ['breaking', 'switch-breaking', 'wire', 'any'],
        help:
            'Exit 1 when a change of this category or a more serious one '
            'is found (any: every change). By default the command only '
            'reports.',
      )
      ..addOption(
        'from',
        help:
            'The lock file to compare from (default: typed_openapi.lock '
            'next to the config).',
      )
      ..addOption(
        'to',
        help:
            'The lock file to compare to, instead of the current spec and '
            'config; needs --from.',
      );
  }

  final StringSink _out;
  final StringSink _err;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  @override
  String get name => 'diff';

  @override
  String get description =>
      'Report how the API of the clients changed since typed_openapi.lock.';

  @override
  String get invocation =>
      '${runner!.executableName} diff [options] [client...]';

  @override
  Future<int> run() async {
    final results = argResults!;
    final diagnostics = DiagnosticBag();
    final (sides, code) = results.option('to') == null
        ? _current(diagnostics)
        : (_files(diagnostics), null);
    _printErrors(diagnostics);
    if (code != null) return code;
    if (sides == null) return CheckCommand.exitHasErrors;
    final changes = {
      for (final MapEntry(key: client, value: (before, after)) in sides.entries)
        client: const ApiDiff().compare(before, after),
    };
    _out.write(switch (results.option('format')) {
      'json' => '${const JsonDiffReport().format(changes)}\n',
      'markdown' => const MarkdownDiffReport().format(changes),
      _ => const TextDiffReport().format(changes),
    });
    final failed =
        _fails(results.option('fail-on'), changes.values.expand((c) => c)) ||
        diagnostics.hasErrors;
    return failed ? CheckCommand.exitHasErrors : ExitCode.success.code;
  }

  /// The sides of each client: the lock (`--from`, or the one next to the
  /// config) and the current spec. The code is set when the config cannot
  /// be loaded.
  (Map<String, (ApiSnapshot, ApiSnapshot)>?, int?) _current(
    DiagnosticBag diagnostics,
  ) {
    final configPath = argResults!.option('config')!;
    final config = ConfigLoader(diagnostics, _paths).load(configPath);
    if (config == null) return (null, ExitCode.config.code);
    final only = argResults!.rest.toSet();
    final unknown = only.difference(config.declaredClients);
    if (unknown.isNotEmpty) {
      usageException(
        'Unknown client: ${(unknown.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
      );
    }
    final from = argResults!.option('from');
    final locked = ApiLock(diagnostics, _paths).read(
      from == null
          ? ApiLock.pathFor(_paths.absolute(configPath))
          : _existing(from),
    );
    final checks = SpecChecker(diagnostics, _paths).check(config, only: only);
    if (locked == null) return (null, null);
    final sides = <String, (ApiSnapshot, ApiSnapshot)>{};
    for (final ClientCheck(:client, :ir) in checks) {
      if (ir == null) {
        _err.writeln(
          'Client ${client.name} has no API: its spec could not be read '
          '(see the errors above).',
        );
        continue;
      }
      sides[client.name] = (
        locked[client.name] ?? const ApiSnapshot({}),
        ApiLockRun.snapshotOf(client, ir),
      );
    }
    return (sides, null);
  }

  /// The sides of each client in the lock files `--from` and `--to`; null
  /// when one cannot be read.
  Map<String, (ApiSnapshot, ApiSnapshot)>? _files(DiagnosticBag diagnostics) {
    final from = argResults!.option('from');
    if (from == null) usageException('--to needs --from.');
    final lock = ApiLock(diagnostics, _paths);
    final before = lock.read(_existing(from));
    final after = lock.read(_existing(argResults!.option('to')!));
    if (before == null || after == null) return null;
    final names = {...before.keys, ...after.keys};
    final only = argResults!.rest.toSet();
    final unknown = only.difference(names);
    if (unknown.isNotEmpty) {
      usageException(
        'No client in either file: ${(unknown.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}.',
      );
    }
    return {
      for (final name in only.isEmpty ? names : only)
        name: (
          before[name] ?? const ApiSnapshot({}),
          after[name] ?? const ApiSnapshot({}),
        ),
    };
  }

  /// The absolute path of the file [path] names; a usage error when there
  /// is none.
  String _existing(String path) {
    final absolute = _paths.absolute(path);
    if (!File(absolute).existsSync()) usageException('No file at $path.');
    return absolute;
  }

  /// Whether a change in [changes] reaches the category [failOn].
  static bool _fails(String? failOn, Iterable<ApiChange> changes) {
    if (failOn == null) return false;
    final limit = ChangeCategory.parse(failOn);
    return changes.any((c) => limit == null || c.category.index <= limit.index);
  }

  /// Writes the errors of [diagnostics] to stderr, with the summary of
  /// every diagnostic: `check` lists the others.
  void _printErrors(DiagnosticBag diagnostics) {
    if (!diagnostics.hasErrors) return;
    final errors = DiagnosticBag();
    diagnostics.all.where((d) => d.severity == .error).forEach(errors.add);
    final listing = const TextReporter().format(errors);
    final summary = TextReporter.summary(errors);
    _err
      ..write(listing.substring(0, listing.length - summary.length))
      ..writeln(TextReporter.summary(diagnostics));
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/json_diff_report.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'api_change.dart';

/// The `json` report of `typed_openapi diff`: per client the count of each
/// category and every change, as [ApiChange.toJson] writes it.
final class JsonDiffReport {
  /// Creates the report.
  const JsonDiffReport();

  /// The report of the changes of each client, by client name.
  String format(Map<String, List<ApiChange>> clients) {
    final names = clients.keys.toList()..sort();
    return const JsonEncoder.withIndent('  ').convert({
      'clients': {
        for (final name in names)
          name: {
            'counts': {
              for (final category in ChangeCategory.values)
                category.label: clients[name]!
                    .where((c) => c.category == category)
                    .length,
            },
            'changes': [for (final c in clients[name]!) c.toJson()],
          },
      },
    });
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/markdown_diff_report.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/message_text.dart';
import 'api_change.dart';
import 'change_counts.dart';

/// The `markdown` report of `typed_openapi diff`, for a pull request
/// comment: a heading per client with the counts and a list of changes
/// per category, a fix in a fenced YAML block. Spec text is neutralised and
/// its Markdown characters escaped, so a spec cannot add a link or an image
/// to the comment.
final class MarkdownDiffReport {
  /// Creates the report.
  const MarkdownDiffReport();

  // The text never starts a line, so only the characters that mark up
  // inline text need an escape; `&` would start an entity.
  static final _special = RegExp(r'[\\`*_\[\]<>|~&]');
  static final _backticks = RegExp('`+');

  /// The report of the changes of each client, by client name.
  String format(Map<String, List<ApiChange>> clients) {
    final out = StringBuffer('## API changes\n');
    for (final name in clients.keys.toList()..sort()) {
      final changes = clients[name]!;
      final counts = changes.isEmpty
          ? 'no changes'
          : '${ChangeCounts(changes)}';
      out.write('\n### ${_text(name)}: $counts\n');
      for (final category in ChangeCategory.values) {
        final listed = changes.where((c) => c.category == category);
        if (listed.isEmpty) continue;
        out.write('\n**${category.label}**\n\n');
        for (final change in listed) {
          out.writeln('- ${_text(change.message)}');
          if (change.fix case final fix?) _writeFix(out, fix);
        }
      }
    }
    return '$out';
  }

  /// Writes [fix] in a fenced block whose fence is longer than any run of
  /// backticks in it, indented to belong to the list item.
  static void _writeFix(StringBuffer out, String fix) {
    final longest = _backticks
        .allMatches(fix)
        .fold(0, (n, m) => m[0]!.length > n ? m[0]!.length : n);
    final fence = '`' * (longest < 3 ? 3 : longest + 1);
    out.write('\n  Keep the old name with this override:\n\n  ${fence}yaml\n');
    for (final line in fix.split('\n')) {
      out.writeln('  ${MessageText.neutralise(line)}');
    }
    out.write('  $fence\n\n');
  }

  static String _text(String text) => MessageText.neutralise(
    text,
  ).replaceAllMapped(_special, (m) => '\\${m[0]}');
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diff/text_diff_report.dart`:

````dart
import '../diagnostics/message_text.dart';
import 'api_change.dart';
import 'change_counts.dart';

/// The `text` report of `typed_openapi diff`: per client a line with the
/// counts, then a line per change and the lines of its fix. Spec text is
/// neutralised, as in the diagnostics report.
final class TextDiffReport {
  /// Creates the report.
  const TextDiffReport();

  /// The report of the changes of each client, by client name.
  String format(Map<String, List<ApiChange>> clients) {
    final out = StringBuffer();
    for (final name in clients.keys.toList()..sort()) {
      final changes = clients[name]!;
      final client = MessageText.neutralise(name);
      if (changes.isEmpty) {
        out.writeln('$client: no changes.');
        continue;
      }
      out.writeln('$client: ${ChangeCounts(changes)}.');
      for (final change in changes) {
        out.writeln(
          '  ${change.category.label}: '
          '${MessageText.neutralise(change.message)}',
        );
        if (change.fix case final fix?) {
          out.writeln('    Keep the old name with this override:');
          for (final line in fix.split('\n')) {
            out.writeln('      ${MessageText.neutralise(line)}');
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return '$out';
  }
}
````

### Task 7: The API lock keeps type and method names (decision 5)

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/CHANGELOG.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/doc/diagnostics.md`

- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/cli/generate_command.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/declaration_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/event_streams.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir_builder.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/ir_context.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/locked_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/method_builder.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/method_names.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/name_lock.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/name_scope.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/schema_mapper.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/stream_method_names.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/api_lock_names.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_builder.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_identities.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_name_lock.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/lib/typed_openapi.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/cli/generate_lock_test.dart`
- Modify: `packages/typed_openapi/test/helpers/ir_fixture.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/name_lock_test.dart`
- Create: `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/name_scope_lock_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: task 1's `MethodNames`; task 3's `ApiSnapshot`, `SnapshotIdentities`; task 5's `ApiLock`, `ApiLockRun`; M6 `DeclarationNames`, `PendingDeclaration`, `SchemaMapper.nameDeclarations`; M10 `EventStreams.methodName`.
- Produces: `NameLock({Uri? root, Map<String, String> types = const {}, Map<String, String> methods = const {}})` (exported, value equality) with `static const none`, `static String locationKey(SpecLocation, Uri root)`, `static String declarationKey(DeclId, Uri root)`, `static String methodKey(String method, String path, {required bool isStream})` (`GET /pets`, `GET /pets#stream`) and `String? typeKey(DeclId)`; `name_scope.dart` with `enum NameChange {reserved, collision, kept, lockTaken}` and `NameScope` (moved from `names.dart`, which exports it) gaining `void lock(Map<String, String> names)` and `(String, NameChange?) claim(String candidate, {List<String> alternatives, String? identity, bool isExplicit = false})`; `LockedNames(IrContext)` with `lockTypes(NameScope, Iterable<PendingDeclaration>)`, `(String, NameChange?) claimType(NameScope, PendingDeclaration, String candidate)`, `lockMethods(NameScope, Iterable<SpecOperation>)`, `(String, NameChange?) claimMethod(NameScope, SpecOperation, String candidate, {required bool isStream, bool isExplicit = false})` (both return the name and a change left to report: TOA570 and TOA569 are reported here); `StreamMethodNames(IrContext).of(SpecOperation, String name, NameScope methods)` (moved from `EventStreams.methodName`); `IrContext(diagnostics, spec, client, {NameLock names = NameLock.none})`; `IrBuilder(DiagnosticBag, {NameLock names = NameLock.none})`; `SnapshotNameLock.of(ApiSnapshot, Uri root) -> NameLock`; `ApiLockNames(DiagnosticBag, DisplayPaths).of(ProjectConfig) -> Map<String, NameLock>`; `SnapshotBuilder.rootOf(ClientConfig) -> Uri`; `const SpecChecker.freshNames(DiagnosticBag, [DisplayPaths])`; `GenerateCommand` option `--fresh-names`; `DiagnosticCode.lockedNameTaken` (`TOA569`, `locked-name-taken`, warning), `lockedNameKept` (`TOA570`, `locked-name-kept`, info), `lockedNameReleased` (`TOA571`, `locked-name-released`, info).

The names are claimed in the same order as before; the lock only decides who may take a name. Before any claim, `LockedNames` locks the type scope with the kept names of the declarations this run has, and each group's method scope with the kept names of its operations (both methods of an operation that also returns events). `NameScope.claim` with an identity whose name is kept gives that name when it is free (`NameChange.kept` when the candidate differs, which `LockedNames` reports as TOA570); a normal claim never takes a name kept for another identity and gets the suffix instead (`collision`); an explicit claim (an override `name`) or `reserve` (a custom type) takes it, and the identity it was kept for falls back to its normal claim (`lockTaken`, TOA569). A kept type that the run does not declare is TOA571 at the document, and its name is free. `SnapshotNameLock` keeps the names of models, enums, unions and methods ("Decisions taken" 6); `SpecChecker` reads the lock for every command and puts the `NameLock` into the run key, so two clients with equal settings but different locks are not one run. `generate --fresh-names` checks with `SpecChecker.freshNames` and writes the fresh names to the lock; its line counts the renames as `breaking`. `NameScope` and the stream method's name move into their own files in this commit so that `names.dart` (which `name_scope.dart` leaves) and `event_streams.dart` (300 lines, parked in the M10 table) do not grow.

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/name_lock_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/diagnostics/diagnostic_code.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/ir.dart';

import '../helpers/ir_fixture.dart';

const _v1 = r'''
openapi: 3.0.3
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /pets:
    get:
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}
''';

/// [_v1] with a component `PetStatus` that `Pet` uses, and an operation
/// whose `operationId` is the name `GET /pets` had.
const _v2 = r'''
openapi: 3.0.3
info: {title: t, version: "1"}
paths:
  /animals:
    get:
      operationId: getPets
      responses:
        '200': {description: ok}
  /pets:
    get:
      responses:
        '200':
          description: ok
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Pet'}
components:
  schemas:
    Pet:
      type: object
      properties:
        status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}
        detail: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/PetStatus'}
    PetStatus:
      type: object
      properties:
        code: {type: integer}
''';

const _inline = '#/components/schemas/Pet/properties/status';
const _component = '#/components/schemas/PetStatus';

String _typeAt(ClientIr ir, String pointer) => ir.declarations
    .firstWhere((d) => d.id.location.pointer.value == pointer)
    .name;

String _methodOf(ClientIr ir, String path) => ir.groups
    .expand((g) => g.operations)
    .firstWhere((o) => o.path == path)
    .name;

void main() {
  final v1 = IrFixture(_v1);
  final lock = v1.lockOf(v1.build());

  test('without the lock a new schema renames an existing type', () {
    final ir = IrFixture(_v2).build();
    expect(_typeAt(ir, _inline), 'PetStatus2');
    expect(_typeAt(ir, _component), 'PetStatus');
  });

  test('adding a schema never renames an existing type with the lock', () {
    final ir = IrFixture(_v2).build(names: lock);
    expect(_typeAt(ir, _inline), 'PetStatus');
    expect(_typeAt(ir, _component), 'PetStatus2');
  });

  test('adding an operation never renames an existing method', () {
    expect(_methodOf(IrFixture(_v2).build(), '/pets'), 'getPets2');
    final ir = IrFixture(_v2).build(names: lock);
    expect(_methodOf(ir, '/pets'), 'getPets');
    expect(_methodOf(ir, '/animals'), 'getPets2');
  });

  test('a kept name a fresh run would not give is TOA570', () {
    final v2 = IrFixture(_v2);
    final kept = v2.lockOf(v2.build(names: lock));
    final v3 = IrFixture(
      _v2.replaceFirst('        status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}\n', ''),
    );
    expect(_typeAt(v3.build(names: kept), _component), 'PetStatus2');
    expect(
      v3.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.lockedNameKept).single.message,
      'typed_openapi.lock keeps the name "PetStatus2" of this type; without '
      'the lock it would be named "PetStatus".',
    );
  });

  test('a type of the lock the spec no longer declares is TOA571', () {
    final v3 = IrFixture(
      _v1.replaceFirst('status: {type: string, enum: [a, b]}', 'name: {}'),
    )..build(names: lock);
    expect(
      v3.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.lockedNameReleased).single.message,
      contains('"PetStatus" for the schema at "$_inline"'),
    );
  });

  test('an override name that takes a kept name is TOA569', () {
    final fixture = IrFixture(
      _v1,
      overrides: 'schemas:\n  Pet:\n    name: PetStatus\n',
    );
    final ir = fixture.build(names: lock);
    expect(_typeAt(ir, '#/components/schemas/Pet'), 'PetStatus');
    expect(_typeAt(ir, _inline), 'PetStatusStatus');
    expect(fixture.reportedOf(DiagnosticCode.lockedNameTaken), hasLength(1));
  });
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/test/ir/name_scope_lock_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:test/test.dart';
import 'package:typed_openapi/src/ir/names.dart';

NameScope _scope() => NameScope(reserved: {'Class'}, reservedSuffix: 'Model');

void main() {
  test('a claim for an identity gets the name the lock keeps', () {
    final scope = _scope()..lock({'a': 'PetStatus2'});
    expect(scope.claim('PetStatus', identity: 'a'), (
      'PetStatus2',
      NameChange.kept,
    ));
  });

  test('the kept name equal to the candidate is no change', () {
    final scope = _scope()..lock({'a': 'Pet'});
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', identity: 'a'), ('Pet', null));
  });

  test('a claim without the identity never takes a kept name', () {
    final scope = _scope()..lock({'a': 'Pet'});
    expect(scope.claim('Pet'), ('Pet2', NameChange.collision));
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', identity: 'b'), ('Pet3', NameChange.collision));
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', identity: 'a'), ('Pet', null));
  });

  test('an explicit claim takes a kept name and the identity is renamed', () {
    final scope = _scope()..lock({'a': 'Pet'});
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', isExplicit: true), ('Pet', null));
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', identity: 'a'), ('Pet2', NameChange.lockTaken));
  });

  test('a reserved name takes a kept name first', () {
    final scope = _scope()
      ..lock({'a': 'Pet'})
      ..reserve('Pet');
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', identity: 'a'), ('Pet2', NameChange.lockTaken));
  });

  test('a kept name says why a fresh run would differ', () {
    final scope = _scope()..lock({'a': 'ClassModel', 'b': 'Pet2', 'c': 'Pet'});
    expect(scope.claim('Class', identity: 'a'), (
      'ClassModel',
      NameChange.reserved,
    ));
    expect(scope.claim('Pet', identity: 'b'), ('Pet2', NameChange.collision));
  });
}
````

The core property (decision 5, "adding a schema never renames an existing type") is `name_lock_test.dart`'s second test, on the IR, and `generate_lock_test.dart`'s `names` group, through the CLI and the lock file.

   switch-breaking | wire | any` sets the exit code. A schema renamed in the
   spec is removed plus added, with the `name:` override that keeps the old
   Dart name (M12).
+- `typed_openapi.lock` keeps the names of models, enums, unions and
+  methods (M12): `check`, `generate`, `diff`, `baseline` and `ir` name each

+  names a kept name a run without the lock would not give, TOA569

   client with it, and a test checks the files equal those of `generate`.

**Diff** (apply from the repository root with `git apply`):

````diff
diff --git a/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md b/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md
index fed0d4a..e87835b 100644
--- a/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md
+++ b/packages/typed_openapi/doc/cli.md
@@ -98,6 +98,7 @@ dart run typed_openapi check --format json | jq '.summary'
 | `--force` | off | Replace files in an output directory that `typed_openapi` did not write or that changed since it wrote them. Each is reported as TOA833. |
 | `--break-lock` | off | Take over a lock when it cannot be told whether the run that holds it still goes. Replaces no file. |
 | `--locked` | off | Check each client against its API snapshot in `typed_openapi.lock` instead of updating the file. A client whose API differs is not written (TOA834). For CI. |
+| `--fresh-names` | off | Name types and methods from the spec alone, not as `typed_openapi.lock` keeps them. Some may be renamed; the change counts say how many. |
 
 ```text
 $ typed_openapi generate
@@ -124,6 +125,8 @@ The second run writes nothing, because the output is the same. Ownership, locks
 
 A run of some clients keeps the sections of the others, and drops the section of a client the config no longer declares. `generate --locked` changes no lock: a client whose API differs from its section, or has none, is skipped with TOA834, and so is a section of a client the config no longer declares. A lock that cannot be read, such as one with merge-conflict markers, is TOA835 and the run writes no client: resolve the conflict, or delete the file to start from the current API. This file is not the lock of an output directory (`.<output>.typed_openapi.lock`), which only keeps two runs apart.
 
+The lock also keeps names. Without it, a schema added to the spec can take the name of a type that existed: a new component `PetStatus` is named before the inline enum `Pet.status`, which was `PetStatus` and would become `PetStatus2`. With the lock, every command that reads the spec (`check`, `generate`, `diff`, `baseline`) names each model, enum, union and method as the lock has it, and the new one gets the suffix (TOA551). A name the lock keeps that a run without it would not give is reported as TOA570 (info); an override name or a custom type that takes a kept name wins, and the type it belonged to is named anew (TOA569, warning). `generate --fresh-names` names everything from the spec alone and writes those names to the lock; the line of each client counts the renames as `breaking`. Fields, enum values, arguments, group classes and the helper types (status types, event unions, parts classes, variant classes) are not kept yet.
+
 ## diff
 
 `typed_openapi diff [options] [client...]` compares the API of each client in `typed_openapi.lock` with the API the current spec and config give, and lists every change with its category. It writes nothing: run it before `generate` to see what a spec update does to code that uses the client. A client the lock does not have reports every type as `additive`; a client whose spec cannot be read is named on stderr and the exit code is `1`.
````

 | TOA566 | `operation-servers-ignored` | warning | A path item or an operation has its own `servers`; the client sends every request to the base URL of the user's transport. | Set the base URL for these requests in an interceptor, or move the servers to the document. |

+| TOA570 | `locked-name-kept` | info | `typed_openapi.lock` keeps the name of a type or a method that a run without the lock would name otherwise, so adding a schema or an operation never renames one that existed. | Run `generate --fresh-names` to take the new names. |
+| TOA571 | `locked-name-released` | info | `typed_openapi.lock` keeps a name for a type the spec no longer declares; the name is free again. | None: the next `generate` drops it from the lock. |

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/locked_names.dart`:

````dart
import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'name_lock.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'pending_declaration.dart';
import 'spec_operation.dart';

/// Claims the names the client's API lock keeps (M12, decision 5), so that
/// adding a schema or an operation never renames a type or a method that
/// existed, and reports where the lock decided: TOA569, TOA570, TOA571.
final class LockedNames {
  /// Claims names for the client of [_context].
  const LockedNames(this._context);

  final IrContext _context;

  NameLock get _lock => _context.names;

  /// Keeps in the library [scope] the names of the declarations [pending];
  /// a type of the lock that is not among them is released (TOA571).
  void lockTypes(NameScope scope, Iterable<PendingDeclaration> pending) {
    final present = {
      for (final declaration in pending) ?_lock.typeKey(declaration.id),
    };
    scope.lock({for (final key in present) key: ?_lock.types[key]});
    for (final MapEntry(:key, :value) in _lock.types.entries) {
      if (present.contains(key)) continue;
      _context.report(
        .lockedNameReleased,
        'typed_openapi.lock keeps the name "$value" for the schema at '
        '"$key", which this run does not declare; the name is free again.',
        _context.model.document.location,
      );
    }
  }

  /// Claims [candidate] in [scope] for the declaration [pending], or the
  /// name the lock keeps for it. A change the lock made is reported and
  /// returned as none.
  (String, NameChange?) claimType(
    NameScope scope,
    PendingDeclaration pending,
    String candidate,
  ) {
    final identity = _lock.typeKey(pending.id);
    final claimed = scope.claim(
      candidate,
      identity: identity,
      isExplicit: pending.overrideName != null,
    );
    return _reported(claimed, candidate, _lock.types[identity], (
      'this type',
      pending.id.location,
    ));
  }

  /// Keeps in a group's method [scope] the names of the operations
  /// [entries] and of their event-stream methods.
  void lockMethods(NameScope scope, Iterable<SpecOperation> entries) {
    scope.lock({
      for (final entry in entries)
        for (final isStream in const [false, true])
          _methodKey(entry, isStream: isStream):
              ?_lock.methods[_methodKey(entry, isStream: isStream)],
    });
  }

  /// Claims [candidate] in [scope] for the method of [entry], its
  /// event-stream method when [isStream], or the name the lock keeps for
  /// it; an [isExplicit] claim (an override's name) ignores the lock. A
  /// change the lock made is reported and returned as none.
  (String, NameChange?) claimMethod(
    NameScope scope,
    SpecOperation entry,
    String candidate, {
    required bool isStream,
    bool isExplicit = false,
  }) {
    final identity = _methodKey(entry, isStream: isStream);
    final claimed = scope.claim(
      candidate,
      identity: identity,
      isExplicit: isExplicit,
    );
    return _reported(claimed, candidate, _lock.methods[identity], (
      'the ${isStream ? 'event-stream ' : ''}method of ${entry.overrideKey}',
      entry.operation.location,
    ));
  }

  static String _methodKey(SpecOperation entry, {required bool isStream}) =>
      NameLock.methodKey(entry.method, entry.path, isStream: isStream);

  /// [claimed], with a change the lock made reported (the [kept] name of
  /// what the [site] names) and returned as none.
  (String, NameChange?) _reported(
    (String, NameChange?) claimed,
    String candidate,
    String? kept,
    (String, SpecLocation) site,
  ) {
    final (name, change) = claimed;
    final (what, at) = site;
    switch (change) {
      case .kept:
        _context.report(
          .lockedNameKept,
          'typed_openapi.lock keeps the name "$name" of $what; without the '
          'lock it would be named "$candidate".',
          at,
          fix: 'Run generate --fresh-names to take the new name.',
        );
        return (name, null);
      case .lockTaken:
        _context.report(
          .lockedNameTaken,
          'typed_openapi.lock keeps the name "$kept" for $what, but an '
          "override's name or a custom type has it; it is named \"$name\".",
          at,
          fix: 'Rename the override or the custom type to keep the name.',
        );
        return (name, null);
      case .reserved || .collision || null:
        return claimed;
    }
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/name_lock.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import '../model/spec_node.dart';
import 'dart_type.dart';

/// The names a client's API lock keeps (M12, decision 5): the name of each
/// declaration by its spec location and of each method by its operation,
/// so that a run keeps them whatever was added to the spec.
final class NameLock {
  /// Creates a lock of [types] and [methods], keyed against the spec whose
  /// root document is [root].
  const NameLock({
    this.root,
    this.types = const {},
    this.methods = const {},
  });

  /// No lock: every name is made from scratch.
  static const none = NameLock();

  /// The root document the keys of [types] are relative to; null for
  /// [none].
  final Uri? root;

  /// The name of each declaration, by [declarationKey].
  final Map<String, String> types;

  /// The name of each method, by [methodKey].
  final Map<String, String> methods;

  /// The identity of [location] in a spec whose root document is [root]:
  /// the file relative to the directory of the root document (empty for
  /// the root document itself), then the JSON pointer.
  static String locationKey(SpecLocation location, Uri root) {
    final document = location.document;
    final file = document == root
        ? ''
        : p.url.relative(document.path, from: p.url.dirname(root.path));
    return '$file${location.pointer.value}';
  }

  /// The identity of the declaration [id]: its location, with `@input` or
  /// `@patch` for the request models of a split schema.
  static String declarationKey(DeclId id, Uri root) => switch (id.variant) {
    .base => locationKey(id.location, root),
    .input => '${locationKey(id.location, root)}@input',
    .patch => '${locationKey(id.location, root)}@patch',
  };

  /// The identity of the method of the operation [method] [path]:
  /// `GET /pets`, with `#stream` for the method that returns its events
  /// next to one that returns its body.
  static String methodKey(
    String method,
    String path, {
    required bool isStream,
  }) => '${method.toUpperCase()} $path${isStream ? '#stream' : ''}';

  /// The identity of the declaration [id] in this lock; null for [none].
  String? typeKey(DeclId id) => switch (root) {
    final root? => declarationKey(id, root),
    null => null,
  };

  String get _key => jsonEncode([
    '${root ?? ''}',
    for (final names in [types, methods])
      [
        for (final key in names.keys.toList()..sort()) [key, names[key]],
      ],
  ]);

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) => other is NameLock && other._key == _key;

  @override
  int get hashCode => _key.hashCode;
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/name_scope.dart`:

````dart
/// Why [NameScope.claim] did not give the candidate itself.
enum NameChange {
  /// The candidate is a reserved word or a name Dart already has.
  reserved,

  /// Another declaration of the scope already has the candidate.
  collision,

  /// The API lock keeps the name the identity had, which is not the
  /// candidate (M12, decision 5).
  kept,

  /// The API lock keeps a name for the identity, but an explicit name (an
  /// override's, or a type of the user) took it first.
  lockTaken,
}

/// The names of one scope (a library's types, a class's members, a
/// method's arguments), given out in the order they are claimed.
///
/// Callers claim in a deterministic order (by spec location), so the same
/// spec always gives the same names. With [lock], a claim for an identity
/// gets the name the API lock keeps for it, and no other claim but an
/// explicit one takes that name.
final class NameScope {
  /// Creates a scope where [reserved] names get [reservedSuffix].
  NameScope({required Set<String> reserved, required this.reservedSuffix})
    : _reserved = reserved;

  final Set<String> _reserved;
  final Set<String> _taken = {};
  final Map<String, String> _kept = {};
  final Map<String, String> _keeper = {};

  /// What a reserved name gets appended: `Model` for types, `_` for
  /// members.
  final String reservedSuffix;

  /// Whether [name] is given out.
  bool isTaken(String name) => _taken.contains(name);

  /// The names given out so far, as a snapshot.
  Set<String> get taken => {..._taken};

  /// Gives [name] to something that is not named in this scope (a type the
  /// user's code declares), so that a later claim of it is a collision.
  void reserve(String name) => _taken.add(name);

  /// Keeps each name of [names] for its identity, the key: only a claim
  /// for that identity, or an explicit one, gets it. Lock only the
  /// identities that are claimed in this run, so a name whose owner is
  /// gone is free again.
  void lock(Map<String, String> names) {
    for (final MapEntry(key: identity, value: name) in names.entries) {
      _kept[identity] = name;
      _keeper[name] = identity;
    }
  }

  /// Gives out [candidate], or a free name made from it: with
  /// [reservedSuffix] when it is reserved, then the first free of
  /// [alternatives], then with `2`, `3`, ... appended. Returns the name and
  /// why it differs from [candidate], if it does.
  ///
  /// A claim for [identity] gets the name [lock] keeps for it while that is
  /// free ([NameChange.kept] when it is not [candidate] for another
  /// reason); an [isExplicit] claim (an override's name) ignores the lock
  /// and may take a name it keeps for another identity.
  (String, NameChange?) claim(
    String candidate, {
    List<String> alternatives = const [],
    String? identity,
    bool isExplicit = false,
  }) {
    final kept = isExplicit ? null : _kept[identity];
    if (kept != null && _taken.add(kept)) {
      return (kept, _keptChange(candidate, kept, identity));
    }
    final owner = isExplicit ? null : identity ?? '';
    final (name, change) = _claim(candidate, alternatives, owner);
    return (name, kept == null ? change : .lockTaken);
  }

  /// Why the [kept] name of [identity] is not [candidate]: what a run
  /// without the lock would report, else [NameChange.kept].
  NameChange? _keptChange(String candidate, String kept, String? identity) {
    if (kept == candidate) return null;
    if (_reserved.contains(candidate)) return .reserved;
    final keeper = _keeper[candidate];
    if (_taken.contains(candidate) || (keeper != null && keeper != identity)) {
      return .collision;
    }
    return .kept;
  }

  /// [candidate] or a free name made from it for [owner]: null for an
  /// explicit claim, which may take any name not given out.
  (String, NameChange?) _claim(
    String candidate,
    List<String> alternatives,
    String? owner,
  ) {
    if (_reserved.contains(candidate)) {
      return (_free('$candidate$reservedSuffix', owner), .reserved);
    }
    if (_take(candidate, owner)) return (candidate, null);
    for (final alternative in alternatives) {
      if (!_reserved.contains(alternative) && _take(alternative, owner)) {
        return (alternative, .collision);
      }
    }
    return (_free(candidate, owner), .collision);
  }

  String _free(String base, String? owner) {
    if (_take(base, owner)) return base;
    for (var i = 2; ; i++) {
      if (_take('$base$i', owner)) return '$base$i';
    }
  }

  /// Gives [name] to [owner] unless it is given out or kept for another
  /// identity.
  bool _take(String name, String? owner) {
    final keeper = _keeper[name];
    if (owner != null && keeper != null && keeper != owner) return false;
    return _taken.add(name);
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/ir/stream_method_names.dart`:

````dart
import 'ir_context.dart';
import 'locked_names.dart';
import 'names.dart';
import 'spec_operation.dart';

/// Names the second method of an operation that returns a body or an
/// event stream (M10, TOA663): the one that returns the events.
final class StreamMethodNames {
  /// Creates the namer for the client of [_context].
  const StreamMethodNames(this._context);

  static final _reservedSuffix = RegExp(r'_+$');

  final IrContext _context;

  /// The name of the second method of an operation named [name] that also
  /// returns a body: [name] with `naming.stream_suffix`, claimed in the
  /// group's [methods] after every other method (TOA663, and TOA550 or
  /// TOA551 when taken), or the name the API lock keeps for it.
  String of(SpecOperation entry, String name, NameScope methods) {
    // A reserved word's suffix (`class_`) gives way to the stream suffix,
    // so the name stays lowerCamelCase (`classStream`).
    final base = name.replaceFirst(_reservedSuffix, '');
    final candidate =
        '${base.isEmpty ? name : base}${_context.config.streamSuffix}';
    final (claimed, change) = LockedNames(
      _context,
    ).claimMethod(methods, entry, candidate, isStream: true);
    final location = entry.operation.location;
    _context.report(
      .eventStreamMethodAdded,
      'This operation returns a body or an event stream; "$name" returns the '
      'body and "$claimed" the events.',
      location,
      fix: 'Set naming.stream_suffix in the config to name it otherwise.',
    );
    if (change != null) {
      _context.report(
        change == .reserved ? .nameReserved : .nameCollision,
        change == .reserved
            ? '"$candidate" is a name Dart already has; the event-stream '
                  'method of ${entry.overrideKey} is named "$claimed".'
            : 'Another method of the group is named "$candidate"; the '
                  'event-stream method of ${entry.overrideKey} is named '
                  '"$claimed".',
        location,
        fix: 'Set naming.stream_suffix in the config, or rename the method.',
      );
    }
    return claimed;
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/api_lock_names.dart`:

````dart
import '../config/project_config.dart';
import '../diagnostics/diagnostic_bag.dart';
import '../diagnostics/display_paths.dart';
import '../ir/name_lock.dart';
import 'api_lock.dart';
import 'snapshot_builder.dart';
import 'snapshot_name_lock.dart';

/// The names `typed_openapi.lock` keeps for the clients of a config (M12,
/// decision 5), which every command that builds an IR names types and
/// methods with.
final class ApiLockNames {
  /// Creates the reader; a lock that cannot be read is reported to [_bag]
  /// (TOA835), naming files as [_paths] does.
  const ApiLockNames(this._bag, this._paths);

  final DiagnosticBag _bag;
  final DisplayPaths _paths;

  /// The names the lock next to [config] keeps, by client name; none when
  /// the file is missing or cannot be read.
  Map<String, NameLock> of(ProjectConfig config) {
    final path = ApiLock.pathFor(_paths.absolute(config.file));
    final clients = ApiLock(_bag, _paths).read(path);
    return {
      for (final client in config.clients)
        if (clients?[client.name] case final snapshot?)
          client.name: SnapshotNameLock.of(
            snapshot,
            SnapshotBuilder.rootOf(client),
          ),
    };
  }
}
````

**Create** `packages/typed_openapi/lib/src/snapshot/snapshot_name_lock.dart`:

````dart
import '../ir/name_lock.dart';
import 'api_snapshot.dart';

/// The [NameLock] an [ApiSnapshot] holds (M12, decision 5): the name of
/// every model, enum and union by its identity, and of every method by its
/// operation's `METHOD /path`.
final class SnapshotNameLock {
  const SnapshotNameLock._();

  static const _stream = '#stream';

  /// The names [snapshot], made for the spec whose root document is
  /// [root], keeps.
  static NameLock of(ApiSnapshot snapshot, Uri root) {
    final types = <String, String>{};
    final methods = <String, String>{};
    for (final MapEntry(key: id, value: type) in snapshot.types.entries) {
      if (_isDeclaration(type.kind)) types[id] = type.name;
      for (final MapEntry(key: memberId, value: member)
          in type.members.entries) {
        if (_methodKey(type, memberId, member) case final key?) {
          methods[key] = member.name;
        }
      }
    }
    return NameLock(root: root, types: types, methods: methods);
  }

  static bool _isDeclaration(TypeKind kind) => switch (kind) {
    .model || .enumeration || .union => true,
    _ => false,
  };

  /// The lock key of the method [member] of [group]; the events method of
  /// an operation that has no other method is its operation's one method.
  static String? _methodKey(
    SnapshotType group,
    String id,
    SnapshotMember member,
  ) {
    if (member.kind != .method) return null;
    final (method, path) = (member.facts['method'], member.facts['path']);
    if (method == null || path == null) return null;
    final isSecond =
        id.endsWith(_stream) &&
        group.members.containsKey(id.substring(0, id.length - _stream.length));
    return NameLock.methodKey(method, path, isStream: isSecond);
  }
}
````
